## 【V3.9.3新增】第十二章：模拟仿真类适配器（12.70-12.75）

### 12.70 MonteCarloSimulator：高级Monte Carlo模拟引擎

#### 12.70.1 适配器概述

MonteCarloSimulator是MetaFramework框架的核弹级模拟工具，支持SS级质量标准下≥100,000次模拟，用于不确定性量化、风险评估和复杂系统仿真。

#### 12.70.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.70: MonteCarloSimulator
功能: 高级Monte Carlo模拟引擎
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.9.3 GA
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Union, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
import warnings

class SimulationType(Enum):
    """模拟类型枚举"""
    PLAIN_MC = "plain"
    ANTITHETIC = "antithetic"
    STRATIFIED = "stratified"
    IMPORTANCE = "importance"
    LATIN_HYPERCUBE = "lhc"

class ConvergenceCriterion(Enum):
    """收敛准则枚举"""
    RELATIVE_ERROR = "relative_error"
    CONFIDENCE_INTERVAL = "ci"
    VARIANCE_COEFFICIENT = "vc"
    MAX_ITERATIONS = "max_iter"

@dataclass
class SimulationConfig:
    """模拟配置"""
    n_simulations: int = 100000
    n_burn_in: int = 1000
    simulation_type: SimulationType = SimulationType.PLAIN_MC
    convergence_criterion: ConvergenceCriterion = ConvergenceCriterion.VARIANCE_COEFFICIENT
    convergence_threshold: float = 0.01
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026
    parallel: bool = True
    n_jobs: int = -1
    confidence_level: float = 0.95
    batch_size: int = 10000

@dataclass
class SimulationResult:
    """模拟结果"""
    estimate: float
    std_error: float
    confidence_interval: Tuple[float, float]
    variance: float
    skewness: Optional[float] = None
    kurtosis: Optional[float] = None
    convergence_history: List[float] = field(default_factory=list)
    effective_sample_size: Optional[int] = None
    simulation_count: int = 0
    execution_time: float = 0.0

class MonteCarloSimulator:
    """
    高级Monte Carlo模拟引擎
    
    功能特性:
    - 支持多种采样策略（朴素、对偶、分层、重要、LHC）
    - 自适应收敛检测
    - 并行计算支持
    - 详细统计分析
    """
    
    MAX_SIMULATIONS = 10000000
    MIN_SIMULATIONS = 100
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[SimulationConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or SimulationConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        self._reset_state()
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
        else:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng()
            
    def _reset_state(self) -> None:
        self.results_cache: Dict[str, SimulationResult] = {}
        self.convergence_data: List[float] = []
        self.iteration_count = 0
        
    def _validate_config(self) -> None:
        if self.config.n_simulations < self.MIN_SIMULATIONS:
            warnings.warn(f"模拟次数过少，将使用最小值")
            self.config.n_simulations = self.MIN_SIMULATIONS
        if self.config.n_simulations > self.MAX_SIMULATIONS:
            warnings.warn(f"模拟次数过多，将使用最大值")
            self.config.n_simulations = self.MAX_SIMULATIONS
        if not 0 < self.config.confidence_level < 1:
            raise ValueError("置信水平必须在(0,1)之间")
            
    def _generate_samples(self, n: int, dim: int = 1) -> np.ndarray:
        method = self.config.simulation_type
        if method == SimulationType.PLAIN_MC:
            return self._plain_mc_samples(n, dim)
        elif method == SimulationType.ANTITHETIC:
            return self._antithetic_samples(n, dim)
        elif method == SimulationType.STRATIFIED:
            return self._stratified_samples(n, dim)
        elif method == SimulationType.LATIN_HYPERCUBE:
            return self._lhc_samples(n, dim)
        else:
            return self._plain_mc_samples(n, dim)
            
    def _plain_mc_samples(self, n: int, dim: int) -> np.ndarray:
        return self.rng.random((n, dim))
        
    def _antithetic_samples(self, n: int, dim: int) -> np.ndarray:
        half_n = n // 2
        U = self.rng.random((half_n, dim))
        return np.vstack([U, 1 - U])
        
    def _stratified_samples(self, n: int, dim: int) -> np.ndarray:
        samples_per_stratum = n // 10
        samples = []
        for i in range(10):
            low, high = i / 10, (i + 1) / 10
            stratum_samples = self.rng.uniform(low, high, (samples_per_stratum, dim))
            samples.append(stratum_samples)
        return np.vstack(samples)
        
    def _lhc_samples(self, n: int, dim: int) -> np.ndarray:
        samples = np.zeros((n, dim))
        for d in range(dim):
            strata = np.linspace(0, 1, n + 1)
            samples[:, d] = self.rng.uniform(strata[:-1], strata[1:])
            self.rng.shuffle(samples[:, d])
        return samples
        
    def _compute_statistics(self, values: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        mean = np.mean(values)
        var = np.var(values, ddof=1)
        std_error = np.sqrt(var / len(values))
        return mean, std_error, var
        
    def _compute_confidence_interval(
        self, mean: float, std_error: float, df: int
    ) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        from scipy import stats
        alpha = 1 - self.config.confidence_level
        t_value = stats.t.ppf(1 - alpha / 2, df)
        margin = t_value * std_error
        return (mean - margin, mean + margin)
        
    def _check_convergence(self, batch_means: List[float], batch_vars: List[float]) -> bool:
        if len(batch_means) < 2:
            return False
        criterion = self.config.convergence_criterion
        threshold = self.config.convergence_threshold
        
        if criterion == ConvergenceCriterion.VARIANCE_COEFFICIENT:
            overall_mean = np.mean(batch_means)
            overall_var = np.mean(batch_vars)
            if abs(overall_mean) < 1e-10:
                vc = np.sqrt(overall_var) / 1e-10
            else:
                vc = np.sqrt(overall_var) / abs(overall_mean)
            return vc < threshold
        return len(batch_means) * self.config.batch_size >= self.config.n_simulations
        
    def simulate(
        self,
        payoff_func: Callable[[np.ndarray], np.ndarray],
        param_distributions: Dict[str, Tuple[str, Tuple]],
        n_simulations: Optional[int] = None,
        **fixed_params
    ) -> SimulationResult:
        import time
        start_time = time.time()
        n = n_simulations or self.config.n_simulations
        self._validate_config()
        
        param_names = list(param_distributions.keys())
        batch_size = self.config.batch_size
        batch_means, batch_vars, all_values = [], [], []
        
        for i in range(0, n, batch_size):
            current_batch = min(batch_size, n - i)
            samples = {
                name: self._sample_from_distribution(dist_type, params, current_batch)
                for name, (dist_type, params) in param_distributions.items()
            }
            param_array = np.column_stack([samples[name] for name in param_names])
            payoffs = payoff_func(param_array, **fixed_params)
            all_values.extend(payoffs.tolist() if hasattr(payoffs, 'tolist') else list(payoffs))
            mean, var = np.mean(payoffs), np.var(payoffs)
            batch_means.append(mean)
            batch_vars.append(var)
            if self._check_convergence(batch_means, batch_vars):
                break
                
        all_values = np.array(all_values)
        final_mean, final_std_error, final_var = self._compute_statistics(all_values)
        
        from scipy import stats
        skewness = float(stats.skew(all_values)) if len(all_values) > 2 else None
        kurtosis = float(stats.kurtosis(all_values)) if len(all_values) > 2 else None
        
        ci = self._compute_confidence_interval(final_mean, final_std_error, len(all_values) - 1)
        execution_time = time.time() - start_time
        
        return SimulationResult(
            estimate=float(final_mean),
            std_error=float(final_std_error),
            confidence_interval=ci,
            variance=float(final_var),
            skewness=skewness,
            kurtosis=kurtosis,
            convergence_history=batch_means,
            effective_sample_size=self._compute_ess(all_values),
            simulation_count=len(all_values),
            execution_time=execution_time
        )
        
    def _sample_from_distribution(
        self, dist_type: str, params: Tuple, n: int
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        if dist_type in ['normal', 'gaussian']:
            mean, std = params
            return self.rng.normal(mean, std, n)
        elif dist_type == 'uniform':
            low, high = params
            return self.rng.uniform(low, high, n)
        elif dist_type == 'exponential':
            scale = params[0] if params else 1.0
            return self.rng.exponential(scale, n)
        elif dist_type == 'lognormal':
            mean, std = params
            return self.rng.lognormal(mean, std, n)
        elif dist_type == 'gamma':
            shape, scale = params
            return self.rng.gamma(shape, scale, n)
        elif dist_type == 'beta':
            alpha, beta_param = params
            return self.rng.beta(alpha, beta_param, n)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported distribution: {dist_type}")
            
    def _compute_ess(self, values: np.ndarray) -> int:
        n = len(values)
        max_lag = min(n // 2, 100)
        acf = self._autocorrelation(values, max_lag)
        cutoff = np.where(np.abs(acf) < 0.05)[0]
        lag_cutoff = cutoff[0] if len(cutoff) > 0 else max_lag
        ess = n / (1 + 2 * np.sum(acf[1:lag_cutoff+1]))
        return int(ess)
        
    def _autocorrelation(self, values: np.ndarray, max_lag: int) -> np.ndarray:
        n = len(values)
        mean, var = np.mean(values), np.var(values)
        if var < 1e-10:
            return np.zeros(max_lag + 1)
        acf = np.zeros(max_lag + 1)
        acf[0] = 1.0
        for lag in range(1, max_lag + 1):
            if n - lag > 0:
                covariance = np.mean((values[:-lag] - mean) * (values[lag:] - mean))
                acf[lag] = covariance / var
        return acf
        
    def simulate_black_scholes(
        self, S: Union[float, np.ndarray], K: float, r: float,
        sigma: float, T: float, option_type: str = 'call'
    ) -> SimulationResult:
        import time
        start_time = time.time()
        Z = self.rng.standard_normal(self.config.n_simulations)
        sigma_Z = sigma * np.sqrt(T) * Z
        drift = (r - 0.5 * sigma**2) * T
        S_T = S * np.exp(drift + sigma_Z)
        
        if option_type.lower() == 'call':
            payoffs = np.maximum(S_T - K, 0)
        else:
            payoffs = np.maximum(K - S_T, 0)
            
        discounted_payoffs = np.exp(-r * T) * payoffs
        mean, std_error, var = self._compute_statistics(discounted_payoffs)
        ci = self._compute_confidence_interval(mean, std_error, len(discounted_payoffs) - 1)
        
        from scipy import stats
        skewness = float(stats.skew(discounted_payoffs))
        kurtosis = float(stats.kurtosis(discounted_payoffs))
        
        return SimulationResult(
            estimate=float(mean),
            std_error=float(std_error),
            confidence_interval=ci,
            variance=float(var),
            skewness=skewness,
            kurtosis=kurtosis,
            convergence_history=[],
            effective_sample_size=len(discounted_payoffs),
            simulation_count=self.config.n_simulations,
            execution_time=time.time() - start_time
        )
        
    def simulate_gbm(
        self, S0: float, mu: float, sigma: float, T: float,
        dt: float = 1/252, n_paths: Optional[int] = None
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        n_steps = int(T / dt)
        n = n_paths or self.config.n_simulations
        Z = self.rng.standard_normal((n_steps, n))
        dt_sqrt = np.sqrt(dt)
        log_returns = (mu - 0.5 * sigma**2) * dt + sigma * dt_sqrt * Z
        log_prices = np.cumsum(log_returns, axis=0)
        S0_array = np.log(S0)
        log_prices = np.vstack([np.full(n, S0_array), log_prices])
        return np.exp(log_prices)
```

#### 12.70.3 使用示例

```python
# 基本使用
mc = MonteCarloSimulator(n_simulations=100000, random_seed=2026)
result = mc.simulate_black_scholes(S=100, K=100, r=0.05, sigma=0.2, T=1)
print(f"期权价格: {result.estimate:.4f}")
print(f"95%置信区间: {result.confidence_interval}")

# 自定义模拟
def custom_payoff(params, a, b):
    return np.sin(params[:, 0] * a) + np.cos(params[:, 1] * b)

result = mc.simulate(
    payoff_func=custom_payoff,
    param_distributions={
        'x': ('normal', (0, 1)),
        'y': ('uniform', (-1, 1))
    },
    a=2.0, b=3.0
)
```

### 12.71 BootstrapValidator：Bootstrap验证框架

#### 12.71.1 适配器概述

BootstrapValidator实现SS级质量标准的≥10,000次Bootstrap验证，用于参数稳定性评估和置信区间计算。

#### 12.71.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.71: BootstrapValidator
功能: Bootstrap验证框架
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.9.3 GA
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from scipy import stats
import warnings

class BootstrapMethod(Enum):
    PERCENTILE = "percentile"
    BCA = "bca"
    BASIC = "basic"
    STUDENTIZED = "studentized"
    WILD = "wild"

@dataclass
class BootstrapConfig:
    n_bootstrap: int = 10000
    confidence_level: float = 0.95
    method: BootstrapMethod = BootstrapMethod.PERCENTILE
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026
    stratified: bool = False
    bias_correct: bool = True
    acceleration: bool = True

@dataclass
class BootstrapResult:
    point_estimate: float
    bias: float
    standard_error: float
    confidence_interval: Tuple[float, float]
    bootstrap_distribution: np.ndarray = field(default_factory=np.array)
    acceleration: Optional[float] = None
    bias_corrected_estimate: Optional[float] = None
    n_effective: int = 0

class BootstrapValidator:
    """
    Bootstrap验证框架
    
    功能特性:
    - 多种Bootstrap方法（百分位、BCa、基本、学生化、野Bootstrap）
    - 自适应偏差校正
    - 分层抽样支持
    - 详细统计报告
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[BootstrapConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or BootstrapConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        self._reset_state()
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
        else:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng()
            
    def _reset_state(self) -> None:
        self.bootstrap_samples: List[np.ndarray] = []
        self.bootstrap_estimates: np.ndarray = np.array([])
        
    def _validate_config(self) -> None:
        if self.config.n_bootstrap < 100:
            warnings.warn(f"Bootstrap次数较少，建议至少1000次")
        if not 0 < self.config.confidence_level < 1:
            raise ValueError("置信水平必须在(0,1)之间")
            
    def validate(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        estimator: Callable[[np.ndarray], float],
        strata: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
    ) -> BootstrapResult:
        self._validate_config()
        data = np.asarray(data)
        point_estimate = estimator(data)
        bootstrap_estimates = self._generate_bootstrap_estimates(data, estimator, strata)
        bias = np.mean(bootstrap_estimates) - point_estimate
        std_error = np.std(bootstrap_estimates, ddof=1)
        ci = self._compute_confidence_interval(point_estimate, bootstrap_estimates, bias)
        
        if self.config.acceleration:
            acceleration = self._compute_acceleration(data, estimator)
        else:
            acceleration = None
            
        return BootstrapResult(
            point_estimate=point_estimate,
            bias=bias,
            standard_error=std_error,
            confidence_interval=ci,
            bootstrap_distribution=bootstrap_estimates,
            acceleration=acceleration,
            n_effective=len(bootstrap_estimates)
        )
        
    def _generate_bootstrap_estimates(
        self, data: np.ndarray, estimator: Callable, strata: Optional[np.ndarray]
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        n = len(data)
        bootstrap_estimates = np.zeros(self.config.n_bootstrap)
        
        if self.config.stratified and strata is not None:
            unique_strata = np.unique(strata)
            strata_indices = {s: np.where(strata == s)[0] for s in unique_strata}
            strata_sizes = {s: len(indices) for s, indices in strata_indices.items()}
            
            for i in range(self.config.n_bootstrap):
                bootstrap_data = []
                for s in unique_strata:
                    indices = strata_indices[s]
                    sampled_indices = self.rng.choice(indices, size=strata_sizes[s], replace=True)
                    bootstrap_data.extend(data[sampled_indices])
                bootstrap_data = np.array(bootstrap_data)
                try:
                    bootstrap_estimates[i] = estimator(bootstrap_data)
                except:
                    bootstrap_estimates[i] = np.nan
        else:
            for i in range(self.config.n_bootstrap):
                indices = self.rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)
                bootstrap_data = data[indices]
                try:
                    bootstrap_estimates[i] = estimator(bootstrap_data)
                except:
                    bootstrap_estimates[i] = np.nan
                    
        return bootstrap_estimates[~np.isnan(bootstrap_estimates)]
        
    def _compute_confidence_interval(
        self, point_estimate: float, bootstrap_estimates: np.ndarray, bias: float
    ) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        alpha = 1 - self.config.confidence_level
        method = self.config.method
        
        if method == BootstrapMethod.PERCENTILE:
            lower = np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, alpha / 2 * 100)
            upper = np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, (1 - alpha / 2) * 100)
        elif method == BootstrapMethod.BCA:
            z0 = stats.norm.ppf(np.mean(bootstrap_estimates <= point_estimate))
            a = self._compute_acceleration(bootstrap_estimates, lambda x: np.mean(x)) if self.config.acceleration else 0
            alpha1 = stats.norm.cdf(z0 + (z0 + stats.norm.ppf(alpha / 2)) / (1 - a * (z0 + stats.norm.ppf(alpha / 2))))
            alpha2 = stats.norm.cdf(z0 + (z0 + stats.norm.ppf(1 - alpha / 2)) / (1 - a * (z0 + stats.norm.ppf(1 - alpha / 2))))
            lower = np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, alpha1 * 100)
            upper = np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, alpha2 * 100)
        elif method == BootstrapMethod.BASIC:
            lower = 2 * point_estimate - np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, (1 - alpha / 2) * 100)
            upper = 2 * point_estimate - np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, alpha / 2 * 100)
        else:
            lower = np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, alpha / 2 * 100)
            upper = np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, (1 - alpha / 2) * 100)
            
        return (float(lower), float(upper))
        
    def _compute_acceleration(self, data: np.ndarray, estimator: Callable) -> float:
        n = len(data)
        jackknife_estimates = np.zeros(n)
        for i in range(n):
            jackknife_data = np.delete(data, i)
            jackknife_estimates[i] = estimator(jackknife_data)
        theta_dot = estimator(data)
        numerator = np.sum((theta_dot - jackknife_estimates) ** 3)
        denominator = 6 * (np.sum((theta_dot - jackknife_estimates) ** 2) ** 1.5)
        return numerator / denominator if denominator > 1e-10 else 0.0
        
    def paired_bootstrap(
        self, data1: np.ndarray, data2: np.ndarray,
        difference_estimator: Callable, n_bootstrap: Optional[int] = None
    ) -> BootstrapResult:
        n1 = len(data1)
        n2 = len(data2)
        if n1 != n2:
            raise ValueError("配对Bootstrap需要相同样本量")
        n = n1
        n_boot = n_bootstrap or self.config.n_bootstrap
        observed_diff = difference_estimator(data1, data2)
        bootstrap_diffs = np.zeros(n_boot)
        for i in range(n_boot):
            indices = self.rng.choice(n, size=n, replace=True)
            bootstrap_diffs[i] = difference_estimator(data1[indices], data2[indices])
        bias = np.mean(bootstrap_diffs) - observed_diff
        std_error = np.std(bootstrap_diffs, ddof=1)
        ci = self._compute_confidence_interval(observed_diff, bootstrap_diffs, bias)
        return BootstrapResult(
            point_estimate=observed_diff,
            bias=bias,
            standard_error=std_error,
            confidence_interval=ci,
            bootstrap_distribution=bootstrap_diffs,
            n_effective=n_boot
        )
        
    def permutation_test(
        self, data1: np.ndarray, data2: np.ndarray,
        test_statistic: Callable, n_permutations: int = 10000
    ) -> Dict:
        observed_stat = test_statistic(data1, data2)
        combined = np.concatenate([data1, data2])
        n1 = len(data1)
        n_total = len(combined)
        perm_stats = np.zeros(n_permutations)
        for i in range(n_permutations):
            perm_indices = self.rng.permutation(n_total)
            perm_data1 = combined[perm_indices[:n1]]
            perm_data2 = combined[perm_indices[n1:]]
            perm_stats[i] = test_statistic(perm_data1, perm_data2)
        p_value = np.mean(np.abs(perm_stats) >= np.abs(observed_stat))
        return {
            'observed_statistic': observed_stat,
            'permutation_distribution': perm_stats,
            'p_value': p_value,
            'n_permutations': n_permutations
        }
```

#### 12.71.3 使用示例

```python
# 基本使用
bv = BootstrapValidator(n_bootstrap=10000, random_seed=2026)
result = bv.validate(data=np.random.randn(100), estimator=np.mean)
print(f"点估计: {result.point_estimate:.4f}")
print(f"偏差: {result.bias:.6f}")
print(f"标准误: {result.standard_error:.6f}")
print(f"95% CI: {result.confidence_interval}")

# 配对Bootstrap
result = bv.paired_bootstrap(
    data1=treatment_group,
    data2=control_group,
    difference_estimator=lambda x, y: np.mean(x) - np.mean(y)
)
```

### 12.72 SurrogateModelBuilder：代理模型构建器

#### 12.72.1 适配器概述

SurrogateModelBuilder构建计算成本高昂模型的代理近似，用于快速评估和不确定性传播。

#### 12.72.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.72: SurrogateModelBuilder
功能: 代理模型构建器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.9.3 GA
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Callable, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from sklearn.gaussian_process import GaussianProcessRegressor
from sklearn.gaussian_process.kernels import RBF, ConstantKernel, WhiteKernel
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor, GradientBoostingRegressor
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from sklearn.model_selection import cross_val_score

class SurrogateType(Enum):
    GAUSSIAN_PROCESS = "gp"
    RANDOM_FOREST = "rf"
    GRADIENT_BOOSTING = "gb"
    POLYNOMIAL = "poly"
    RADIAL_BASIS = "rbf"

@dataclass
class SurrogateConfig:
    model_type: SurrogateType = SurrogateType.GAUSSIAN_PROCESS
    n_training_samples: int = 1000
    test_size: float = 0.2
    cross_validation_folds: int = 5
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026
    scale_inputs: bool = True
    scale_outputs: bool = True
    hyperparameters: Dict = field(default_factory=dict)
    
class SurrogateModel:
    def __init__(self, model, scaler_x, scaler_y):
        self.model = model
        self.scaler_x = scaler_x
        self.scaler_y = scaler_y
        
    def predict(self, X: np.ndarray, return_std: bool = False):
        X_scaled = self.scaler_x.transform(X)
        y_scaled = self.model.predict(X_scaled)
        y = self.scaler_y.inverse_transform(y_scaled.reshape(-1, 1)).flatten()
        if return_std and hasattr(self.model, 'predict'):
            try:
                y_std_scaled = self.model.predict(X_scaled, return_std=True)[1]
                y_std = y_std_scaled * self.scaler_y.scale_
                return y, y_std
            except:
                pass
        return y
        
    def evaluate(self, X_test: np.ndarray, y_test: np.ndarray) -> Dict:
        y_pred = self.predict(X_test)
        mse = np.mean((y_pred - y_test) ** 2)
        rmse = np.sqrt(mse)
        mae = np.mean(np.abs(y_pred - y_test))
        ss_res = np.sum((y_test - y_pred) ** 2)
        ss_tot = np.sum((y_test - np.mean(y_test)) ** 2)
        r2 = 1 - ss_res / (ss_tot + 1e-10)
        return {'mse': mse, 'rmse': rmse, 'mae': mae, 'r2': r2}

@dataclass
class SurrogateResult:
    model: SurrogateModel
    training_score: float
    test_score: Optional[float]
    cv_scores: List[float]
    feature_importance: Optional[np.ndarray] = None

class SurrogateModelBuilder:
    """
    代理模型构建器
    
    功能特性:
    - 多种代理模型类型
    - 自动超参数调优
    - 交叉验证评估
    - 不确定性估计
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[SurrogateConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or SurrogateConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
            
    def build(
        self,
        X_train: np.ndarray,
        y_train: np.ndarray,
        high_fidelity_func: Optional[Callable] = None,
        X_test: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
        y_test: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
    ) -> SurrogateResult:
        if self.config.scale_inputs:
            self.scaler_x = StandardScaler()
            X_train_scaled = self.scaler_x.fit_transform(X_train)
        else:
            self.scaler_x = None
            X_train_scaled = X_train
            
        if self.config.scale_outputs:
            self.scaler_y = StandardScaler()
            y_train_scaled = self.scaler_y.fit_transform(y_train.reshape(-1, 1)).flatten()
        else:
            self.scaler_y = None
            y_train_scaled = y_train
            
        if high_fidelity_func is not None and len(X_train) < self.config.n_training_samples:
            X_train_scaled, y_train_scaled = self._expand_training_data(
                X_train, y_train, high_fidelity_func
            )
            
        model = self._create_model()
        model.fit(X_train_scaled, y_train_scaled)
        training_score = model.score(X_train_scaled, y_train_scaled)
        
        test_score = None
        if X_test is not None and y_test is not None:
            if self.config.scale_inputs:
                X_test_scaled = self.scaler_x.transform(X_test)
            else:
                X_test_scaled = X_test
            test_score = model.score(X_test_scaled, y_test)
            
        cv_scores = cross_val_score(
            model, X_train_scaled, y_train_scaled,
            cv=self.config.cross_validation_folds, scoring='r2'
        ).tolist()
        
        feature_importance = self._get_feature_importance(model, X_train.shape[1])
        surrogate = SurrogateModel(model, self.scaler_x, self.scaler_y)
        
        return SurrogateResult(
            model=surrogate,
            training_score=training_score,
            test_score=test_score,
            cv_scores=cv_scores,
            feature_importance=feature_importance
        )
        
    def _expand_training_data(
        self, X_train: np.ndarray, y_train: np.ndarray, high_fidelity_func: Callable
    ) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        n_needed = self.config.n_training_samples - len(X_train)
        if n_needed > 0:
            X_new = self._lhs_samples(n_needed, X_train.shape[1], X_train)
            y_new = high_fidelity_func(X_new)
            X_train = np.vstack([X_train, X_new])
            y_train = np.concatenate([y_train, y_new])
        if self.config.scale_inputs:
            X_train_scaled = self.scaler_x.transform(X_train)
        else:
            X_train_scaled = X_train
        if self.config.scale_outputs:
            y_train_scaled = self.scaler_y.fit_transform(y_train.reshape(-1, 1)).flatten()
        else:
            y_train_scaled = y_train
        return X_train_scaled, y_train_scaled
        
    def _lhs_samples(self, n: int, dim: int, reference_data: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        bounds = np.column_stack([np.min(reference_data, axis=0), np.max(reference_data, axis=0)])
        samples = np.zeros((n, dim))
        for d in range(dim):
            low, high = bounds[d]
            strata = np.linspace(0, 1, n + 1)
            samples[:, d] = self.rng.uniform(strata[:-1], strata[1:])
            self.rng.shuffle(samples[:, d])
            samples[:, d] = samples[:, d] * (high - low) + low
        return samples
        
    def _create_model(self):
        model_type = self.config.model_type
        params = self.config.hyperparameters
        
        if model_type == SurrogateType.GAUSSIAN_PROCESS:
            kernel = params.get('kernel', ConstantKernel(1.0) * RBF(1.0) + WhiteKernel())
            return GaussianProcessRegressor(
                kernel=kernel,
                alpha=params.get('alpha', 1e-10),
                n_restarts_optimizer=params.get('n_restarts_optimizer', 5),
                random_state=self.config.random_seed
            )
        elif model_type == SurrogateType.RANDOM_FOREST:
            return RandomForestRegressor(
                n_estimators=params.get('n_estimators', 100),
                max_depth=params.get('max_depth', 10),
                random_state=self.config.random_seed
            )
        elif model_type == SurrogateType.GRADIENT_BOOSTING:
            return GradientBoostingRegressor(
                n_estimators=params.get('n_estimators', 100),
                max_depth=params.get('max_depth', 3),
                learning_rate=params.get('learning_rate', 0.1),
                random_state=self.config.random_seed
            )
        else:
            return GaussianProcessRegressor(random_state=self.config.random_seed)
            
    def _get_feature_importance(self, model, n_features: int) -> Optional[np.ndarray]:
        if hasattr(model, 'feature_importances_'):
            return model.feature_importances_
        elif hasattr(model, 'coef_'):
            return np.abs(model.coef_)
        return None
        
    def build_adaptive(
        self,
        X_initial: np.ndarray,
        y_initial: np.ndarray,
        high_fidelity_func: Callable,
        max_iterations: int = 10,
        points_per_iteration: int = 50
    ) -> List[SurrogateResult]:
        X_train = X_initial.copy()
        y_train = y_initial.copy()
        results = []
        
        for iteration in range(max_iterations):
            result = self.build(X_train, y_train)
            results.append(result)
            X_candidate = self._lhs_samples(
                points_per_iteration * 2, X_train.shape[1], X_train
            )
            _, y_std = result.model.predict(X_candidate, return_std=True)
            if y_std is None:
                y_std = np.ones(len(X_candidate))
            n_select = min(points_per_iteration, len(X_candidate))
            selected_indices = np.argsort(y_std)[-n_select:]
            X_new = X_candidate[selected_indices]
            y_new = high_fidelity_func(X_new)
            X_train = np.vstack([X_train, X_new])
            y_train = np.concatenate([y_train, y_new])
            
        return results
```

#### 12.72.3 使用示例

```python
# 构建代理模型
builder = SurrogateModelBuilder(n_training_samples=500, random_seed=2026)
result = builder.build(X_train, y_train, high_fidelity_func=expensive_simulation)
metrics = result.model.evaluate(X_test, y_test)
print(f"R²: {metrics['r2']:.4f}")
print(f"RMSE: {metrics['rmse']:.6f}")

# 预测
y_pred = result.model.predict(X_new)
y_pred, y_std = result.model.predict(X_new, return_std=True)
```

### 12.73 SensitivityAnalyzer：全局敏感性分析器

#### 12.73.1 适配器概述

SensitivityAnalyzer实现基于Sobol指数和微分方法的全局敏感性分析，识别关键参数和不确定性来源。

#### 12.73.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.73: SensitivityAnalyzer
功能: 全局敏感性分析器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.9.3 GA
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum

class SensitivityMethod(Enum):
    SOBOL = "sobol"
    FAST = "fast"
    MORRIS = "morris"
    DERIVATIVE = "derivative"
    REGRESSION = "regression"

@dataclass
class SensitivityConfig:
    method: SensitivityMethod = SensitivityMethod.SOBOL
    n_samples: int = 10000
    n_bootstrap: int = 1000
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026

@dataclass
class SensitivityResult:
    first_order_indices: Dict[str, float]
    total_order_indices: Dict[str, float]
    interaction_indices: Optional[Dict[Tuple[str, str], float]] = None
    parameter_names: List[str]
    model_output_variance: float
    confidence_intervals: Optional[Dict] = None

class SensitivityAnalyzer:
    """
    全局敏感性分析器
    
    功能特性:
    - Sobol' 全局敏感性分析
    - Morris筛选法
    - 参数导数分析
    - 不确定性传播
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[SensitivityConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or SensitivityConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
            
    def analyze(
        self,
        model_func: Callable[[np.ndarray], np.ndarray],
        param_bounds: Dict[str, Tuple[float, float]],
        param_names: Optional[List[str]] = None
    ) -> SensitivityResult:
        if param_names is None:
            param_names = list(param_bounds.keys())
        n_params = len(param_names)
        bounds_array = np.array([param_bounds[name] for name in param_names])
        
        method = self.config.method
        if method == SensitivityMethod.SOBOL:
            return self._sobol_analysis(model_func, bounds_array, param_names)
        elif method == SensitivityMethod.MORRIS:
            return self._morris_analysis(model_func, bounds_array, param_names)
        elif method == SensitivityMethod.DERIVATIVE:
            return self._derivative_analysis(model_func, bounds_array, param_names)
        elif method == SensitivityMethod.REGRESSION:
            return self._regression_analysis(model_func, bounds_array, param_names)
        else:
            return self._sobol_analysis(model_func, bounds_array, param_names)
            
    def _sobol_analysis(
        self, model_func: Callable, bounds: np.ndarray, param_names: List[str]
    ) -> SensitivityResult:
        n_params = len(param_names)
        n_samples = self.config.n_samples
        
        A = self._random_sample(n_samples, bounds)
        B = self._random_sample(n_samples, bounds)
        Y_A = model_func(A)
        Y_B = model_func(B)
        total_variance = np.var(np.concatenate([Y_A, Y_B]))
        
        first_order = {}
        total_order = {}
        interaction = {}
        
        for i in range(n_params):
            A_B_i = A.copy()
            A_B_i[:, i] = B[:, i]
            Y_A_B_i = model_func(A_B_i)
            var_i = np.mean(Y_B * (Y_A_B_i - Y_A)) / total_variance
            first_order[param_names[i]] = float(max(0, var_i))
            var_T_i = 1 - np.mean(Y_A * (Y_A - Y_A_B_i)) / total_variance
            total_order[param_names[i]] = float(max(0, var_T_i))
            
            for j in range(i + 1, n_params):
                A_B_ij = A.copy()
                A_B_ij[:, i] = B[:, i]
                A_B_ij[:, j] = B[:, j]
                Y_A_B_ij = model_func(A_B_ij)
                V_ij = np.mean(Y_A * (Y_A_B_ij - Y_A_B_i - Y_A_B_j + Y_A)) / total_variance
                interaction[(param_names[i], param_names[j])] = float(max(0, V_ij))
                
        return SensitivityResult(
            first_order_indices=first_order,
            total_order_indices=total_order,
            interaction_indices=interaction,
            parameter_names=param_names,
            model_output_variance=float(total_variance)
        )
        
    def _random_sample(self, n: int, bounds: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        n_params = bounds.shape[0]
        samples = self.rng.random((n, n_params))
        for i in range(n_params):
            low, high = bounds[i]
            samples[:, i] = samples[:, i] * (high - low) + low
        return samples
        
    def _morris_analysis(
        self, model_func: Callable, bounds: np.ndarray, param_names: List[str]
    ) -> SensitivityResult:
        n_params = len(param_names)
        n_trajectories = max(10, self.config.n_samples // (n_params + 1))
        
        normalized_bounds = np.zeros_like(bounds)
        for i in range(n_params):
            normalized_bounds[i, 0] = 0
            normalized_bounds[i, 1] = 1
            
        elementary_effects = []
        for traj in range(n_trajectories):
            x = self.rng.random(n_params)
            order = self.rng.permutation(n_params)
            for i in order:
                x_plus = x.copy()
                delta = self.rng.choice([-1, 1]) * 0.1
                x_plus[i] = min(1, max(0, x_plus[i] + delta))
                y = model_func(self._unnormalize(x.reshape(1, -1), bounds))[0]
                y_plus = model_func(self._unnormalize(x_plus.reshape(1, -1), bounds))[0]
                ee = (y_plus - y) / delta
                elementary_effects.append((param_names[i], ee))
                x = x_plus
                
        first_order = {}
        total_order = {}
        for name in param_names:
            effects = [ee for n, ee in elementary_effects if n == name]
            first_order[name] = float(np.mean(np.abs(effects)))
            total_order[name] = float(np.std(effects))
            
        return SensitivityResult(
            first_order_indices=first_order,
            total_order_indices=total_order,
            parameter_names=param_names,
            model_output_variance=0.0
        )
        
    def _unnormalize(self, X: np.ndarray, bounds: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
        X_unnorm = np.zeros_like(X)
        for i in range(X.shape[1]):
            low, high = bounds[i]
            X_unnorm[:, i] = X[:, i] * (high - low) + low
        return X_unnorm
        
    def _derivative_analysis(
        self, model_func: Callable, bounds: np.ndarray, param_names: List[str]
    ) -> SensitivityResult:
        baseline = np.mean(bounds, axis=1)
        h = 0.001
        derivatives = {}
        for i in range(len(param_names)):
            x_plus = baseline.copy()
            x_minus = baseline.copy()
            x_plus[i] += h
            x_minus[i] -= h
            y_plus = model_func(x_plus.reshape(1, -1))[0]
            y_minus = model_func(x_minus.reshape(1, -1))[0]
            derivatives[param_names[i]] = float((y_plus - y_minus) / (2 * h))
            
        y_baseline = model_func(baseline.reshape(1, -1))[0]
        first_order = {}
        for name, deriv in derivatives.items():
            idx = param_names.index(name)
            param_value = baseline[idx]
            if abs(param_value) > 1e-10:
                elasticity = deriv * param_value / y_baseline
            else:
                elasticity = abs(deriv)
            first_order[name] = float(elasticity)
            
        return SensitivityResult(
            first_order_indices=first_order,
            total_order_indices=first_order.copy(),
            parameter_names=param_names,
            model_output_variance=0.0
        )
        
    def _regression_analysis(
        self, model_func: Callable, bounds: np.ndarray, param_names: List[str]
    ) -> SensitivityResult:
        n_samples = self.config.n_samples
        X = self._random_sample(n_samples, bounds)
        Y = model_func(X)
        
        X_mean, X_std = np.mean(X, axis=0), np.std(X, axis=0)
        X_norm = (X - X_mean) / (X_std + 1e-10)
        
        from numpy.linalg import lstsq
        coeffs, _, _, _ = lstsq(X_norm, Y, rcond=None)
        
        first_order = {
            param_names[i]: float(abs(coeffs[i]))
            for i in range(len(param_names))
        }
        
        return SensitivityResult(
            first_order_indices=first_order,
            total_order_indices=first_order.copy(),
            parameter_names=param_names,
            model_output_variance=float(np.var(Y))
        )
        
    def rank_parameters(self, result: SensitivityResult, by: str = 'first_order') -> List[Tuple[str, float]]:
        indices = result.first_order_indices if by == 'first_order' else result.total_order_indices
        return sorted(indices.items(), key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)
        
    def identify_critical_parameters(self, result: SensitivityResult, threshold: float = 0.1) -> List[str]:
        critical = []
        for name in result.parameter_names:
            if (result.first_order_indices.get(name, 0) >= threshold or
                result.total_order_indices.get(name, 0) >= threshold):
                critical.append(name)
        return critical
```

#### 12.73.3 使用示例

```python
# Sobol敏感性分析
sa = SensitivityAnalyzer(n_samples=10000, random_seed=2026)
result = sa.analyze(
    model_func=complex_simulation,
    param_bounds={
        'alpha': (0.1, 0.5),
        'beta': (0.2, 0.8),
        'gamma': (0.5, 1.5)
    }
)
print("第一阶指数:", result.first_order_indices)
print("总阶指数:", result.total_order_indices)

ranked = sa.rank_parameters(result, by='first_order')
print("关键参数:", ranked)
```

### 12.74 UncertaintyQuantifier：不确定性量化器

#### 12.74.1 适配器概述

UncertaintyQuantifier提供完整的不确定性量化框架，包括概率分布估计、矩估计和传播分析。

#### 12.74.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.74: UncertaintyQuantifier
功能: 不确定性量化器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.9.3 GA
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Callable, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from scipy import stats

class DistributionType(Enum):
    NORMAL = "normal"
    LOGNORMAL = "lognormal"
    UNIFORM = "uniform"
    TRIANGULAR = "triangular"
    BETA = "beta"
    GAMMA = "gamma"
    EMPIRICAL = "empirical"

@dataclass
class UncertaintyConfig:
    confidence_level: float = 0.95
    n_bootstrap: int = 5000
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026
    kde_bandwidth: Optional[str] = "scott"

@dataclass
class UncertaintyResult:
    point_estimate: float
    variance: float
    standard_deviation: float
    confidence_interval: Tuple[float, float]
    distribution_type: DistributionType
    distribution_params: Dict
    skewness: Optional[float] = None
    kurtosis: Optional[float] = None
    percentiles: Dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)

class UncertaintyQuantifier:
    """
    不确定性量化器
    
    功能特性:
    - 参数不确定性量化
    - 概率分布拟合
    - 置信区间估计
    - 矩估计
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[UncertaintyConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or UncertaintyConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
            
    def quantify(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        distribution_type: Union[str, DistributionType] = 'empirical',
        params: Optional[Dict] = None
    ) -> UncertaintyResult:
        data = np.asarray(data)
        if isinstance(distribution_type, str):
            dist_type = DistributionType(distribution_type)
        else:
            dist_type = distribution_type
            
        point_estimate = float(np.mean(data))
        variance = self._bootstrap_variance(data)
        std_dev = float(np.sqrt(variance))
        ci = self._compute_confidence_interval(data, dist_type, params)
        skewness = float(stats.skew(data))
        kurtosis = float(stats.kurtosis(data))
        percentiles = self._compute_percentiles(data)
        dist_params = self._fit_distribution(data, dist_type)
        
        return UncertaintyResult(
            point_estimate=point_estimate,
            variance=variance,
            standard_deviation=std_dev,
            confidence_interval=ci,
            distribution_type=dist_type,
            distribution_params=dist_params,
            skewness=skewness,
            kurtosis=kurtosis,
            percentiles=percentiles
        )
        
    def _bootstrap_variance(self, data: np.ndarray) -> float:
        n = len(data)
        n_boot = self.config.n_bootstrap
        bootstrap_means = np.zeros(n_boot)
        for i in range(n_boot):
            sample = self.rng.choice(data, size=n, replace=True)
            bootstrap_means[i] = np.mean(sample)
        return float(np.var(bootstrap_means, ddof=1))
        
    def _compute_confidence_interval(
        self, data: np.ndarray, dist_type: DistributionType, params: Optional[Dict]
    ) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        alpha = 1 - self.config.confidence_level
        if dist_type == DistributionType.EMPIRICAL:
            lower = np.percentile(data, alpha / 2 * 100)
            upper = np.percentile(data, (1 - alpha / 2) * 100)
        else:
            dist_params = params or self._fit_distribution(data, dist_type)
            if dist_type == DistributionType.NORMAL:
                mean, std = dist_params['mean'], dist_params['std']
                z = stats.norm.ppf(1 - alpha / 2)
                lower, upper = mean - z * std, mean + z * std
            elif dist_type == DistributionType.LOGNORMAL:
                mu, sigma = dist_params['mu'], dist_params['sigma']
                log_lower = mu - stats.norm.ppf(1 - alpha / 2) * sigma
                log_upper = mu + stats.norm.ppf(1 - alpha / 2) * sigma
                lower, upper = np.exp(log_lower), np.exp(log_upper)
            else:
                lower = np.percentile(data, alpha / 2 * 100)
                upper = np.percentile(data, (1 - alpha / 2) * 100)
        return (float(lower), float(upper))
        
    def _compute_percentiles(self, data: np.ndarray) -> Dict[int, float]:
        percentiles_to_compute = [5, 10, 25, 50, 75, 90, 95]
        return {p: float(np.percentile(data, p)) for p in percentiles_to_compute}
        
    def _fit_distribution(self, data: np.ndarray, dist_type: DistributionType) -> Dict:
        if dist_type == DistributionType.NORMAL:
            return {'mean': float(np.mean(data)), 'std': float(np.std(data, ddof=1))}
        elif dist_type == DistributionType.LOGNORMAL:
            log_data = np.log(data)
            return {'mu': float(np.mean(log_data)), 'sigma': float(np.std(log_data, ddof=1))}
        elif dist_type == DistributionType.UNIFORM:
            return {'low': float(np.min(data)), 'high': float(np.max(data))}
        elif dist_type == DistributionType.BETA:
            mean, var = np.mean(data), np.var(data)
            a = mean * (mean * (1 - mean) / var - 1)
            b = (1 - mean) * (mean * (1 - mean) / var - 1)
            return {'a': float(a), 'b': float(b)}
        elif dist_type == DistributionType.GAMMA:
            mean, var = np.mean(data), np.var(data)
            shape = mean**2 / var
            scale = var / mean
            return {'shape': float(shape), 'scale': float(scale)}
        return {}
        
    def propagate_uncertainty(
        self,
        input_uncertainty: Dict[str, UncertaintyResult],
        model_func: Callable[[np.ndarray], float],
        n_monte_carlo: int = 10000
    ) -> UncertaintyResult:
        param_names = list(input_uncertainty.keys())
        n_params = len(param_names)
        samples = np.zeros((n_monte_carlo, n_params))
        
        for i, name in enumerate(param_names):
            uq_result = input_uncertainty[name]
            dist_type = uq_result.distribution_type
            params = uq_result.distribution_params
            samples[:, i] = self._sample_from_distribution(dist_type, params, n_monte_carlo)
            
        output_samples = np.array([model_func(samples[j]) for j in range(n_monte_carlo)])
        return self.quantify(output_samples, distribution_type='empirical')
        
    def _sample_from_distribution(
        self, dist_type: DistributionType, params: Dict, n: int
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        if dist_type == DistributionType.NORMAL:
            return self.rng.normal(params['mean'], params['std'], n)
        elif dist_type == DistributionType.LOGNORMAL:
            return self.rng.lognormal(params['mu'], params['sigma'], n)
        elif dist_type == DistributionType.UNIFORM:
            return self.rng.uniform(params['low'], params['high'], n)
        elif dist_type == DistributionType.EMPIRICAL:
            return self.rng.choice(params['data'], size=n, replace=True)
        else:
            return self.rng.normal(params.get('mean', 0), params.get('std', 1), n)
            
    def kde_quantify(self, data: np.ndarray, n_points: int = 500) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        kde = stats.gaussian_kde(data, bw_method=self.config.kde_bandwidth)
        x_min, x_max = np.min(data) - 3 * np.std(data), np.max(data) + 3 * np.std(data)
        x = np.linspace(x_min, x_max, n_points)
        pdf = kde(x)
        return x, pdf
        
    def compare_distributions(self, data1: np.ndarray, data2: np.ndarray, test: str = 'ks') -> Dict:
        if test == 'ks':
            statistic, p_value = stats.ks_2samp(data1, data2)
        else:
            statistic, p_value = stats.ttest_ind(data1, data2)
        return {
            'statistic': float(statistic),
            'p_value': float(p_value),
            'test': test,
            'significant': p_value < 0.05
        }
```

#### 12.74.3 使用示例

```python
# 不确定性量化
uq = UncertaintyQuantifier(n_bootstrap=5000, random_seed=2026)
result = uq.quantify(data, distribution_type='empirical')
print(f"点估计: {result.point_estimate:.4f}")
print(f"标准差: {result.standard_deviation:.4f}")
print(f"95% CI: {result.confidence_interval}")

# 不确定性传播
output_uq = uq.propagate_uncertainty(
    input_uncertainty={'alpha': alpha_uq, 'beta': beta_uq},
    model_func=complex_model
)
```

### 12.75 DeepEnsemblePredictor：深度集成预测器

#### 12.75.1 适配器概述

DeepEnsemblePredictor实现多模型深度集成预测，结合多种预测方法提升准确性和鲁棒性。

#### 12.75.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.75: DeepEnsemblePredictor
功能: 深度集成预测器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.9.3 GA
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Callable, Union
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from sklearn.linear_model import LinearRegression, Ridge, Lasso
from sklearn.ensemble import RandomForestRegressor, GradientBoostingRegressor
from sklearn.neural_network import MLPRegressor
from sklearn.preprocessing import StandardScaler
from sklearn.model_selection import cross_val_score
import warnings

class EnsembleMethod(Enum):
    SIMPLE_AVERAGE = "simple_average"
    WEIGHTED_AVERAGE = "weighted_average"
    STACKING = "stacking"
    BAGGING = "bagging"
    BOOSTING = "boosting"

@dataclass
class EnsembleConfig:
    method: EnsembleMethod = EnsembleMethod.WEIGHTED_AVERAGE
    base_models: List[str] = field(default_factory=lambda: ['linear', 'rf', 'gb', 'nn'])
    n_folds: int = 5
    test_size: float = 0.2
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026
    optimize_weights: bool = True
    stacking_meta_learner: str = 'ridge'
    scale_features: bool = True

@dataclass
class EnsembleResult:
    predictions: np.ndarray
    prediction_std: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
    model_weights: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None
    individual_predictions: Optional[Dict[str, np.ndarray]] = None
    ensemble_score: float = 0.0
    individual_scores: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None
    feature_importance: Optional[Dict[str, float]] = None

class BaseModel:
    def __init__(self, name: str, model, scaler: Optional[StandardScaler] = None):
        self.name = name
        self.model = model
        self.scaler = scaler
        
    def fit(self, X: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> 'BaseModel':
        if self.scaler is not None:
            X_scaled = self.scaler.fit_transform(X)
        else:
            X_scaled = X
        self.model.fit(X_scaled, y)
        return self
        
    def predict(self, X: np.ndarray, return_std: bool = False):
        if self.scaler is not None:
            X_scaled = self.scaler.transform(X)
        else:
            X_scaled = X
        y_pred = self.model.predict(X_scaled)
        
        if return_std and hasattr(self.model, 'estimators_'):
            preds = np.array([est.predict(X_scaled) for est in self.model.estimators_])
            return np.mean(preds, axis=0), np.std(preds, axis=0)
        return y_pred, None

class DeepEnsemblePredictor:
    """
    深度集成预测器
    
    功能特性:
    - 多模型集成（线性、树、神经网络）
    - 自适应权重优化
    - Stacking元学习
    - 预测不确定性估计
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[EnsembleConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or EnsembleConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        self._initialize_models()
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            np.random.seed(self.config.random_seed)
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
            
    def _initialize_models(self) -> None:
        self.models: Dict[str, BaseModel] = {}
        self.model_scores: Dict[str, float] = {}
        for model_name in self.config.base_models:
            model, scaler = self._create_model(model_name)
            self.models[model_name] = BaseModel(model_name, model, scaler)
            
    def _create_model(self, model_name: str) -> Tuple:
        if model_name == 'linear':
            return LinearRegression(), (StandardScaler() if self.config.scale_features else None)
        elif model_name == 'ridge':
            return Ridge(alpha=1.0, random_state=self.config.random_seed), (StandardScaler() if self.config.scale_features else None)
        elif model_name == 'lasso':
            return Lasso(alpha=0.1, random_state=self.config.random_seed), (StandardScaler() if self.config.scale_features else None)
        elif model_name == 'rf':
            return RandomForestRegressor(n_estimators=100, max_depth=10, random_state=self.config.random_seed), None
        elif model_name == 'gb':
            return GradientBoostingRegressor(n_estimators=100, max_depth=5, learning_rate=0.1, random_state=self.config.random_seed), None
        elif model_name == 'nn':
            return MLPRegressor(hidden_layer_sizes=(100, 50), max_iter=500, random_state=self.config.random_seed, early_stopping=True), (StandardScaler() if self.config.scale_features else None)
        else:
            return LinearRegression(), (StandardScaler() if self.config.scale_features else None)
            
    def fit(
        self,
        X_train: np.ndarray,
        y_train: np.ndarray,
        X_val: Optional[np.ndarray] = None,
        y_val: Optional[np.ndarray] = None
    ) -> 'DeepEnsemblePredictor':
        individual_predictions = {}
        for name, model_wrapper in self.models.items():
            try:
                model_wrapper.fit(X_train, y_train)
                if len(X_train) >= self.config.n_folds * 2:
                    scores = cross_val_score(
                        model_wrapper.model,
                        model_wrapper.scaler.transform(X_train) if model_wrapper.scaler else X_train,
                        y_train, cv=self.config.n_folds, scoring='neg_mean_squared_error'
                    )
                    score = -np.mean(scores)
                else:
                    pred, _ = model_wrapper.predict(X_train)
                    score = np.mean((pred - y_train) ** 2)
                self.model_scores[name] = float(score)
                if X_val is not None:
                    pred, _ = model_wrapper.predict(X_val)
                    individual_predictions[name] = pred
            except Exception as e:
                warnings.warn(f"模型 {name} 训练失败: {e}")
                self.model_scores[name] = float('inf')
                
        if self.config.optimize_weights:
            self._optimize_weights(X_train, y_train, X_val, y_val)
        else:
            self._equal_weights()
            
        return self
        
    def _equal_weights(self) -> None:
        n_models = len([m for m, s in self.model_scores.items() if s < float('inf')])
        weight = 1.0 / n_models if n_models > 0 else 0
        self.weights = {name: weight if score < float('inf') else 0 for name, score in self.model_scores.items()}
        
    def _optimize_weights(
        self, X_train: np.ndarray, y_train: np.ndarray,
        X_val: Optional[np.ndarray], y_val: Optional[np.ndarray]
    ) -> None:
        method = self.config.method
        
        if method == EnsembleMethod.SIMPLE_AVERAGE:
            self._equal_weights()
        elif method == EnsembleMethod.WEIGHTED_AVERAGE:
            valid_scores = {n: s for n, s in self.model_scores.items() if s < float('inf')}
            if len(valid_scores) == 0:
                self._equal_weights()
                return
            inv_scores = {n: 1.0 / s for n, s in valid_scores.items()}
            total = sum(inv_scores.values())
            self.weights = {name: inv_scores.get(name, 0) / total for name in self.models.keys()}
        elif method == EnsembleMethod.STACKING:
            self._stacking_weights(X_train, y_train)
        else:
            self._equal_weights()
            
    def _stacking_weights(self, X: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray) -> None:
        from sklearn.model_selection import KFold
        n_models = len(self.models)
        n_samples = len(y)
        meta_features = np.zeros((n_samples, n_models))
        
        kf = KFold(n_splits=self.config.n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=self.config.random_seed)
        for fold, (train_idx, val_idx) in enumerate(kf.split(X)):
            X_tr, X_va = X[train_idx], X[val_idx]
            y_tr = y[train_idx]
            for i, (name, model_wrapper) in enumerate(self.models.items()):
                try:
                    model_wrapper.fit(X_tr, y_tr)
                    pred, _ = model_wrapper.predict(X_va)
                    meta_features[val_idx, i] = pred
                except:
                    meta_features[val_idx, i] = np.mean(y_tr)
                    
        if self.config.stacking_meta_learner == 'ridge':
            meta_model = Ridge(alpha=1.0)
        else:
            meta_model = LinearRegression()
            
        meta_model.fit(meta_features, y)
        
        if hasattr(meta_model, 'coef_'):
            coefs = np.abs(meta_model.coef_)
            coefs = coefs / (np.sum(coefs) + 1e-10)
            self.weights = {name: float(coefs[i]) if i < len(coefs) else 0 for i, name in enumerate(self.models.keys())}
        else:
            self._equal_weights()
            
        self.meta_model = meta_model
        
    def predict(
        self, X: np.ndarray, return_uncertainty: bool = True
    ) -> EnsembleResult:
        individual_predictions = {}
        weighted_predictions = np.zeros(len(X))
        total_weight = sum(self.weights.values()) + 1e-10
        
        for name, model_wrapper in self.models.items():
            weight = self.weights.get(name, 0) / total_weight
            if weight > 0:
                try:
                    pred, std = model_wrapper.predict(X, return_std=True)
                    individual_predictions[name] = pred
                    weighted_predictions += weight * pred
                except Exception as e:
                    warnings.warn(f"模型 {name} 预测失败: {e}")
                    
        if return_uncertainty and len(individual_predictions) > 1:
            pred_array = np.column_stack(list(individual_predictions.values()))
            prediction_std = np.std(pred_array, axis=1)
        else:
            prediction_std = None
            
        ensemble_score = np.mean([s for s in self.model_scores.values() if s < float('inf')]) if self.model_scores else 0
        feature_importance = self._compute_feature_importance(X)
        
        return EnsembleResult(
            predictions=weighted_predictions,
            prediction_std=prediction_std,
            model_weights=self.weights,
            individual_predictions=individual_predictions,
            ensemble_score=ensemble_score,
            individual_scores=self.model_scores,
            feature_importance=feature_importance
        )
        
    def _compute_feature_importance(self, X: np.ndarray) -> Optional[Dict[str, float]]:
        importance_dict = {}
        for name, model_wrapper in self.models.items():
            model = model_wrapper.model
            if hasattr(model, 'feature_importances_'):
                importance_dict[name] = {f'feature_{i}': float(imp) for i, imp in enumerate(model.feature_importances_)}
            elif hasattr(model, 'coef_'):
                importance_dict[name] = {f'feature_{i}': float(abs(c)) for i, c in enumerate(model.coef_)}
                
        if len(importance_dict) == 0:
            return None
            
        n_features = len(X[0])
        return {f'feature_{i}': np.mean([imp.get(f'feature_{i}', 0) for imp in importance_dict.values()]) for i in range(n_features)}
        
    def add_model(self, name: str, model_class: Callable, **model_kwargs) -> 'DeepEnsemblePredictor':
        instance = model_class(**model_kwargs)
        scaler = StandardScaler() if self.config.scale_features else None
        self.models[name] = BaseModel(name, instance, scaler)
        self.model_scores[name] = float('inf')
        self.weights[name] = 0
        return self
        
    def get_diversity_score(self) -> float:
        if len(self.models) < 2:
            return 0.0
        correlations = []
        model_names = list(self.models.keys())
        for i in range(len(model_names)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(model_names)):
                corr = 1.0 - abs(self.weights.get(model_names[i], 0) - self.weights.get(model_names[j], 0))
                correlations.append(corr)
        return float(np.mean(correlations)) if correlations else 0.0
        
    def cross_validate(self, X: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, n_folds: int = 5) -> Dict:
        from sklearn.model_selection import KFold
        fold_scores = []
        kf = KFold(n_splits=n_folds, shuffle=True, random_state=self.config.random_seed)
        
        for fold, (train_idx, val_idx) in enumerate(kf.split(X)):
            X_tr, X_va = X[train_idx], X[val_idx]
            y_tr, y_va = y[train_idx], y[val_idx]
            self.fit(X_tr, y_tr)
            result = self.predict(X_va)
            mse = np.mean((result.predictions - y_va) ** 2)
            fold_scores.append(mse)
            
        return {
            'fold_scores': fold_scores,
            'mean_score': float(np.mean(fold_scores)),
            'std_score': float(np.std(fold_scores))
        }
```

#### 12.75.3 使用示例

```python
# 深度集成预测
predictor = DeepEnsemblePredictor()
predictor.fit(X_train, y_train)

# 预测
result = predictor.predict(X_test)
print(f"集成预测: {result.predictions[:5]}")
print(f"预测不确定性: {result.prediction_std[:5]}")
print(f"模型权重: {result.model_weights}")

# 添加自定义模型
predictor.add_model('xgb', GradientBoostingRegressor, n_estimators=200, max_depth=5)

# 交叉验证
cv_results = predictor.cross_validate(X, y, n_folds=5)
print(f"CV MSE: {cv_results['mean_score']:.4f} +/- {cv_results['std_score']:.4f}")
```

### 12.76 HierarchicalThresholdCalibrator：分层临界值校准器 ⭐ V3.10.0新增

#### 12.76.1 适配器概述

HierarchicalThresholdCalibrator实现V3.4.3第3.14.0节的分层临界值体系校准，支持宏观/中观/微观三层差异化临界值的验证与应用。

**核心参数（来自V3.4.3）**：

| 层级 | 临界值符号 | 数值 | 验证方法 | 实证基础 |
|------|-----------|------|----------|----------|
| 宏观层 | $\xi_c^{macro}$ | $0.12 \pm 0.02$ | 反身性增益验证，$R^2 = 0.92$ | 历史数据拟合 |
| 中观层 | $\xi_c^{meso}$ | $0.25$ | 理论推导值 | 理论标定 |
| 微观层 | $\xi_c^{micro}$ | $0.33 \pm 0.03$ | 门限回归验证 | CFPS数据估计 |

**理论依据**: V3.4.3第2497-2519行

#### 12.76.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.76: HierarchicalThresholdCalibrator
功能: 分层临界值校准器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.10.0-Alpha.2
理论依据: V3.4.3第3.14.0节分层临界值体系
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Literal
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from scipy import stats
from scipy.optimize import minimize_scalar

@dataclass
class ThresholdConfig:
    """分层临界值配置"""
    # 宏观层临界值（国家/社会层面）
    xi_c_macro: float = 0.12
    xi_c_macro_std: float = 0.02
    
    # 中观层临界值（行业/组织层面）
    xi_c_meso: float = 0.25
    xi_c_meso_std: float = 0.0  # 理论推导，无不确定性
    
    # 微观层临界值（家庭/个人层面）
    xi_c_micro: float = 0.33
    xi_c_micro_std: float = 0.03
    
    # 改革反身性增益函数参数
    beta: float = 2.5  # 敏感性参数
    gamma_0: float = 0.5  # 基础增益
    gamma_1: float = 1.0  # 调制幅度
    
    # 置信水平
    confidence_level: float = 0.95
    
    # Bootstrap重采样次数
    n_bootstrap: int = 10000
    
    # 随机种子
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026

@dataclass
class CalibrationResult:
    """校准结果"""
    layer: str
    xi_c: float
    ci_lower: float
    ci_upper: float
    r_squared: Optional[float]
    validation_method: str
    is_valid: bool
    confidence: float

class HierarchicalThresholdCalibrator:
    """
    分层临界值校准器
    
    功能特性:
    - 三层临界值校准（宏观/中观/微观）
    - Bootstrap置信区间估计
    - 改革反身性增益计算
    - 临界状态验证
    
    理论依据: V3.4.3第3.14.0节
    
    核心公式:
    - 分层临界值: ξ_c^(scale) ∈ {ξ_c^macro, ξ_c^meso, ξ_c^micro}
    - 改革增益: γ(ξ) = γ_0 + γ_1·tanh[β·(ξ_c^scale - ξ)]
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[ThresholdConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or ThresholdConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        self._calibration_history: List[Dict] = []
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        """初始化随机数生成器"""
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
        else:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng()
    
    def get_threshold(
        self, 
        layer: Literal['macro', 'meso', 'micro']
    ) -> Tuple[float, float, float]:
        """
        获取指定层级的临界值
        
        Args:
            layer: 层级 ('macro', 'meso', 'micro')
            
        Returns:
            (xi_c, ci_lower, ci_upper)
        """
        if layer == 'macro':
            xi_c = self.config.xi_c_macro
            ci_lower = xi_c - 1.96 * self.config.xi_c_macro_std
            ci_upper = xi_c + 1.96 * self.config.xi_c_macro_std
        elif layer == 'meso':
            xi_c = self.config.xi_c_meso
            ci_lower = xi_c
            ci_upper = xi_c
        else:  # micro
            xi_c = self.config.xi_c_micro
            ci_lower = xi_c - 1.96 * self.config.xi_c_micro_std
            ci_upper = xi_c + 1.96 * self.config.xi_c_micro_std
            
        return xi_c, ci_lower, ci_upper
    
    def calibrate_threshold(
        self,
        layer: Literal['macro', 'meso', 'micro'],
        data: np.ndarray,
        validation_method: str = 'bootstrap'
    ) -> CalibrationResult:
        """
        校准指定层级临界值
        
        Args:
            layer: 层级
            data: 历史数据（改革强度序列）
            validation_method: 验证方法 ('bootstrap', 'regression', 'theoretical')
            
        Returns:
            校准结果
        """
        xi_c_base, ci_lower_base, ci_upper_base = self.get_threshold(layer)
        
        if validation_method == 'bootstrap':
            # Bootstrap重采样估计置信区间
            bootstrap_estimates = self._bootstrap_calibration(data, layer)
            xi_c_calibrated = float(np.mean(bootstrap_estimates))
            ci_lower = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, 2.5))
            ci_upper = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_estimates, 97.5))
            r_squared = self._compute_r_squared(data, xi_c_calibrated)
            
        elif validation_method == 'regression':
            # 门限回归估计
            xi_c_calibrated, r_squared = self._threshold_regression(data)
            std_error = self._compute_std_error(data, xi_c_calibrated)
            ci_lower = xi_c_calibrated - 1.96 * std_error
            ci_upper = xi_c_calibrated + 1.96 * std_error
            
        else:  # theoretical
            xi_c_calibrated = xi_c_base
            ci_lower = ci_lower_base
            ci_upper = ci_upper_base
            r_squared = None
        
        # 验证校准结果
        is_valid = self._validate_threshold(xi_c_calibrated, layer)
        confidence = self._compute_confidence(r_squared, validation_method)
        
        result = CalibrationResult(
            layer=layer,
            xi_c=xi_c_calibrated,
            ci_lower=ci_lower,
            ci_upper=ci_upper,
            r_squared=r_squared,
            validation_method=validation_method,
            is_valid=is_valid,
            confidence=confidence
        )
        
        # 记录校准历史
        self._calibration_history.append({
            'layer': layer,
            'xi_c': xi_c_calibrated,
            'validation_method': validation_method,
            'r_squared': r_squared,
            'is_valid': is_valid
        })
        
        return result
    
    def _bootstrap_calibration(
        self, 
        data: np.ndarray, 
        layer: str,
        n_bootstrap: Optional[int] = None
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """Bootstrap重采样校准"""
        n_boot = n_bootstrap or self.config.n_bootstrap
        n = len(data)
        
        if n < 10:
            return np.array([self.get_threshold(layer)[0]])
        
        bootstrap_estimates = np.zeros(n_boot)
        
        for i in range(n_boot):
            # 有放回重采样
            indices = self.rng.integers(0, n, size=n)
            bootstrap_sample = data[indices]
            
            # 估计临界值（简化：使用样本均值）
            bootstrap_estimates[i] = np.mean(bootstrap_sample)
        
        return bootstrap_estimates
    
    def _threshold_regression(
        self, 
        data: np.ndarray
    ) -> Tuple[float, float]:
        """门限回归估计临界值"""
        # 简化的门限回归实现
        # 实际应用中应使用TRAMO/TVAR等完整实现
        
        sorted_data = np.sort(data)
        n = len(sorted_data)
        
        # 网格搜索最优门限
        best_sse = np.inf
        best_threshold = np.mean(sorted_data)
        
        for i in range(int(n * 0.2), int(n * 0.8)):
            candidate = sorted_data[i]
            
            # 计算分段回归残差
            below = data[data < candidate]
            above = data[data >= candidate]
            
            if len(below) > 2 and len(above) > 2:
                sse = np.sum((below - np.mean(below))**2) + \
                      np.sum((above - np.mean(above))**2)
                
                if sse < best_sse:
                    best_sse = sse
                    best_threshold = candidate
        
        # 计算R²
        total_ss = np.sum((data - np.mean(data))**2)
        r_squared = 1 - best_sse / total_ss if total_ss > 0 else 0
        
        return best_threshold, r_squared
    
    def _compute_r_squared(
        self, 
        data: np.ndarray, 
        xi_c: float
    ) -> float:
        """计算R²"""
        # 简化的R²计算
        # 实际应用中应使用完整的反身性增益模型拟合
        
        residuals = data - xi_c
        ss_res = np.sum(residuals**2)
        ss_tot = np.sum((data - np.mean(data))**2)
        
        if ss_tot == 0:
            return 0.0
        
        return 1 - ss_res / ss_tot
    
    def _compute_std_error(
        self, 
        data: np.ndarray, 
        xi_c: float
    ) -> float:
        """计算标准误"""
        n = len(data)
        if n <= 1:
            return 0.0
        return np.std(data, ddof=1) / np.sqrt(n)
    
    def _validate_threshold(
        self, 
        xi_c: float, 
        layer: str
    ) -> bool:
        """验证临界值有效性"""
        base_xi_c, ci_lower, ci_upper = self.get_threshold(layer)
        
        # 检查校准值是否在基准值置信区间内
        if layer == 'meso':
            # 中观层为理论值，精确匹配
            return abs(xi_c - base_xi_c) < 0.05
        else:
            return ci_lower <= xi_c <= ci_upper
    
    def _compute_confidence(
        self, 
        r_squared: Optional[float], 
        validation_method: str
    ) -> float:
        """计算置信度"""
        if validation_method == 'theoretical':
            return 0.70
        elif r_squared is None:
            return 0.50
        else:
            # R²越高，置信度越高
            return min(0.99, 0.50 + 0.49 * r_squared)
    
    def validate_reform_intensity(
        self,
        xi: float,
        layer: Literal['macro', 'meso', 'micro']
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        验证改革强度是否超过临界
        
        Args:
            xi: 改革强度 [0, 1]
            layer: 分析层级
            
        Returns:
            验证结果字典
        """
        xi_c, ci_lower, ci_upper = self.get_threshold(layer)
        
        # 计算韧性余量
        resilience_margin = xi_c - xi
        
        # 判断状态
        if xi < ci_lower:
            status = 'below_critical'
            phase = '改革安全区'
            gain = self.get_reform_gain(xi, layer)
        elif xi > ci_upper:
            status = 'above_critical'
            phase = '改革陷阱区'
            gain = self.get_reform_gain(xi, layer)
        else:
            status = 'at_critical'
            phase = '改革临界区'
            gain = self.get_reform_gain(xi, layer)
        
        return {
            'xi': xi,
            'xi_c': xi_c,
            'ci_lower': ci_lower,
            'ci_upper': ci_upper,
            'resilience_margin': resilience_margin,
            'status': status,
            'phase': phase,
            'gain': gain,
            'is_above_critical': xi > xi_c
        }
    
    def get_reform_gain(
        self,
        xi: float,
        layer: Optional[Literal['macro', 'meso', 'micro']] = None,
        xi_c: Optional[float] = None,
        beta: Optional[float] = None,
        gamma_0: Optional[float] = None,
        gamma_1: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> float:
        """
        计算改革反身性增益
        
        核心公式: γ(ξ) = γ_0 + γ_1·tanh[β·(ξ_c - ξ)]
        
        Args:
            xi: 改革强度
            layer: 层级（可选，提供时自动使用对应参数）
            xi_c: 临界值（可选）
            beta: 敏感性参数
            gamma_0: 基础增益
            gamma_1: 调制幅度
            
        Returns:
            改革反身性增益
        """
        # 确定参数来源
        if layer is not None:
            xi_c_actual, _, _ = self.get_threshold(layer)
        elif xi_c is not None:
            xi_c_actual = xi_c
        else:
            xi_c_actual = self.config.xi_c_meso  # 默认使用中观层
        
        beta_actual = beta if beta is not None else self.config.beta
        gamma_0_actual = gamma_0 if gamma_0 is not None else self.config.gamma_0
        gamma_1_actual = gamma_1 if gamma_1 is not None else self.config.gamma_1
        
        # 计算增益
        gain = gamma_0_actual + gamma_1_actual * np.tanh(beta_actual * (xi_c_actual - xi))
        
        return float(gain)
    
    def get_layer_guidance(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """获取层级适用指南"""
        return {
            'macro': {
                'description': '国家/社会层面制度改革',
                'typical_scenarios': ['政治体制改革', '经济制度转型', '法律体系重构'],
                'xi_c': self.config.xi_c_macro,
                'uncertainty': self.config.xi_c_macro_std,
                'validation': '历史数据拟合，R²=0.92'
            },
            'meso': {
                'description': '行业/组织变革',
                'typical_scenarios': ['企业战略转型', '行业标准制定', '市场竞争格局变化'],
                'xi_c': self.config.xi_c_meso,
                'uncertainty': 0.0,
                'validation': '理论推导值'
            },
            'micro': {
                'description': '家庭/个人决策',
                'typical_scenarios': ['家庭财富配置', '个人职业选择', '消费行为决策'],
                'xi_c': self.config.xi_c_micro,
                'uncertainty': self.config.xi_c_micro_std,
                'validation': '门限回归，CFPS数据'
            }
        }
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """生成校准报告"""
        report_lines = [
            "=" * 60,
            "分层临界值校准报告",
            "=" * 60,
            "",
            "【宏观层临界值】",
            f"  ξ_c^macro = {self.config.xi_c_macro:.4f} ± {self.config.xi_c_macro_std:.4f}",
            f"  95% CI: [{self.config.xi_c_macro - 1.96*self.config.xi_c_macro_std:.4f}, "
            f"{self.config.xi_c_macro + 1.96*self.config.xi_c_macro_std:.4f}]",
            "",
            "【中观层临界值】",
            f"  ξ_c^meso = {self.config.xi_c_meso:.4f} (理论值)",
            "",
            "【微观层临界值】",
            f"  ξ_c^micro = {self.config.xi_c_micro:.4f} ± {self.config.xi_c_micro_std:.4f}",
            f"  95% CI: [{self.config.xi_c_micro - 1.96*self.config.xi_c_micro_std:.4f}, "
            f"{self.config.xi_c_micro + 1.96*self.config.xi_c_micro_std:.4f}]",
            "",
            f"校准历史记录: {len(self._calibration_history)} 次",
            "=" * 60
        ]
        return "\n".join(report_lines)
```

#### 12.76.3 使用示例

```python
# 分层临界值校准
from hierarchical_threshold_calibrator import HierarchicalThresholdCalibrator

# 初始化
calibrator = HierarchicalThresholdCalibrator()

# 获取各层临界值
for layer in ['macro', 'meso', 'micro']:
    xi_c, ci_lower, ci_upper = calibrator.get_threshold(layer)
    print(f"{layer}: ξ_c = {xi_c:.4f}, CI = [{ci_lower:.4f}, {ci_upper:.4f}]")

# 校准宏观层临界值（使用历史数据）
historical_data = np.array([0.05, 0.08, 0.12, 0.15, 0.18, 0.22])
result = calibrator.calibrate_threshold('macro', historical_data, method='bootstrap')
print(f"宏观层校准结果: ξ_c = {result.xi_c:.4f}, R² = {result.r_squared:.4f}")

# 验证改革强度
validation = calibrator.validate_reform_intensity(xi=0.10, layer='macro')
print(f"改革状态: {validation['phase']}, 增益: {validation['gain']:.4f}")

# 计算改革增益曲线
xi_range = np.linspace(0, 0.5, 50)
gains = [calibrator.get_reform_gain(xi, 'macro') for xi in xi_range]
print(f"改革增益范围: [{min(gains):.4f}, {max(gains):.4f}]")

# 获取层级指南
guidance = calibrator.get_layer_guidance()
print(f"宏观层适用场景: {guidance['macro']['typical_scenarios']}")
```
