### 12.77 UrbanizationPhaseDetector：城镇化相变检测器 ⭐ V3.10.0新增

#### 12.77.1 适配器概述

UrbanizationPhaseDetector实现V3.4.3第3.12.4节的城镇化相变检测，检测城镇化率是否突破相变临界点。

**核心参数（来自V3.4.3）**：

| 参数 | 符号 | 数值 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|------|
| 城镇化相变临界值 | $u_c$ | $44.3\% \pm 2\%$ | Bootstrap CI |
| 城镇化-改革耦合系数 | $\alpha_u$ | 待标定 | 耦合方程参数 |

**理论依据**: V3.4.3第2328-2350行

#### 12.77.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.77: UrbanizationPhaseDetector
功能: 城镇化相变检测器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.10.0-Alpha.2
理论依据: V3.4.3第3.12.4节城镇化相变临界值
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Literal
from dataclasses import dataclass
from scipy import stats
from scipy.optimize import curve_fit

@dataclass
class UrbanizationConfig:
    """城镇化检测配置"""
    # 城镇化相变临界值
    u_c: float = 0.443  # 44.3%
    u_c_std: float = 0.02  # ±2%
    
    # 城镇化-改革耦合系数
    alpha_u: float = 0.5
    
    # Bootstrap参数
    n_bootstrap: int = 10000
    confidence_level: float = 0.95
    
    # 相变年份预测参数
    prediction_horizon: int = 10  # 预测未来10年
    
    # 随机种子
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026

@dataclass
class PhaseDetectionResult:
    """相变检测结果"""
    urbanization_rate: float
    is_above_critical: bool
    distance_to_critical: float  # 距临界点的距离
    phase: str  # 'pre_transition', 'at_transition', 'post_transition'
    confidence: float
    confidence_interval: Tuple[float, float]

class UrbanizationPhaseDetector:
    """
    城镇化相变检测器
    
    功能特性:
    - 城镇化率相变临界点检测
    - Bootstrap置信区间估计
    - 城镇化-改革耦合效应计算
    - 相变年份预测
    
    理论依据: V3.4.3第3.12.4节
    
    核心公式:
    - 相变临界值: u_c = 44.3% ± 2%
    - 耦合方程: ∂ξ/∂t ⊃ α_u·(u - u_c)·ξ
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[UrbanizationConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or UrbanizationConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        self._detection_history: List[Dict] = []
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        """初始化随机数生成器"""
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
        else:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng()
    
    def detect_phase(
        self, 
        urbanization_rate: float,
        use_bootstrap: bool = True
    ) -> PhaseDetectionResult:
        """
        检测城镇化率是否突破相变临界点
        
        Args:
            urbanization_rate: 城镇化率 [0, 1]
            use_bootstrap: 是否使用Bootstrap估计置信区间
            
        Returns:
            相变检测结果
        """
        # 计算距临界点的距离
        distance = urbanization_rate - self.config.u_c
        
        # 确定相阶段
        if urbanization_rate < self.config.u_c - self.config.u_c_std:
            phase = 'pre_transition'  # 城镇化前阶段
        elif urbanization_rate > self.config.u_c + self.config.u_c_std:
            phase = 'post_transition'  # 城镇化后阶段
        else:
            phase = 'at_transition'  # 相变临界区
        
        # 计算置信区间
        if use_bootstrap:
            ci_lower = self.config.u_c - 1.96 * self.config.u_c_std
            ci_upper = self.config.u_c + 1.96 * self.config.u_c_std
            confidence = 0.95
        else:
            ci_lower = self.config.u_c
            ci_upper = self.config.u_c
            confidence = 0.70
        
        result = PhaseDetectionResult(
            urbanization_rate=urbanization_rate,
            is_above_critical=urbanization_rate > self.config.u_c,
            distance_to_critical=distance,
            phase=phase,
            confidence=confidence,
            confidence_interval=(ci_lower, ci_upper)
        )
        
        # 记录检测历史
        self._detection_history.append({
            'urbanization_rate': urbanization_rate,
            'phase': phase,
            'is_above_critical': result.is_above_critical
        })
        
        return result
    
    def coupling_effect(
        self,
        u: float,
        xi: float,
        alpha_u: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        计算城镇化-改革耦合效应
        
        耦合方程: ∂ξ/∂t ⊃ α_u·(u - u_c)·ξ
        
        Args:
            u: 城镇化率
            xi: 改革强度
            alpha_u: 耦合系数（可选）
            
        Returns:
            耦合效应字典
        """
        alpha = alpha_u if alpha_u is not None else self.config.alpha_u
        
        # 计算城镇化偏差
        u_delta = u - self.config.u_c
        
        # 计算耦合效应
        coupling_effect = alpha * u_delta * xi
        
        # 判断耦合方向
        if u > self.config.u_c:
            direction = 'positive'  # 城镇化红利释放
            interpretation = '改革推动力增强'
        elif u < self.config.u_c:
            direction = 'negative'  # 传统惯性阻力
            interpretation = '改革阻力增大'
        else:
            direction = 'neutral'
            interpretation = '处于相变临界点'
        
        return {
            'u': u,
            'u_c': self.config.u_c,
            'u_delta': u_delta,
            'xi': xi,
            'alpha_u': alpha,
            'coupling_effect': coupling_effect,
            'direction': direction,
            'interpretation': interpretation
        }
    
    def phase_transition_year(
        self,
        urbanization_data: np.ndarray,
        years: np.ndarray,
        method: str = 'linear_extrapolation'
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        预测城镇化相变年份
        
        Args:
            urbanization_data: 历史城镇化率序列
            years: 对应年份
            method: 预测方法
            
        Returns:
            预测结果字典
        """
        if len(urbanization_data) < 3:
            return {
                'status': 'insufficient_data',
                'message': '需要至少3个数据点进行预测',
                'predicted_year': None,
                'confidence': 0.0
            }
        
        if method == 'linear_extrapolation':
            # 线性外推
            slope, intercept, r_value, p_value, std_err = stats.linregress(
                years, urbanization_data
            )
            
            # 求解 u(t) = u_c
            if slope > 0:
                t_transition = (self.config.u_c - intercept) / slope
                predicted_year = int(np.round(t_transition))
            else:
                predicted_year = None
            
            # 计算置信区间（简化）
            if predicted_year is not None:
                # 基于标准误估计不确定性
                year_uncertainty = std_err / abs(slope) * 1.96 if slope != 0 else 0
                year_ci_lower = predicted_year - year_uncertainty
                year_ci_upper = predicted_year + year_uncertainty
            else:
                year_ci_lower = year_ci_upper = None
            
            r_squared = r_value ** 2
            
        elif method == 'logistic_growth':
            # Logistic增长模型
            def logistic(t, L, k, t0):
                return L / (1 + np.exp(-k * (t - t0)))
            
            try:
                # 初始参数估计
                L0 = 1.0  # 最大城镇化率
                k0 = 0.1  # 增长率
                t0_0 = years[np.argmax(urbanization_data > 0.4)] if any(urbanization_data > 0.4) else years[len(years)//2]
                
                popt, _ = curve_fit(
                    logistic, years, urbanization_data,
                    p0=[L0, k0, t0_0],
                    bounds=([0.5, 0.01, years[0]], [1.0, 1.0, years[-1] + 20]),
                    maxfev=5000
                )
                
                L, k, t0 = popt
                
                # 求解 u(t) = u_c
                if k > 0:
                    t_transition = t0 + np.log((1 - self.config.u_c/L) / (self.config.u_c/L)) / k
                    predicted_year = int(np.round(t_transition))
                else:
                    predicted_year = None
                
                year_ci_lower = year_ci_upper = None
                r_squared = 1 - np.sum((logistic(years, *popt) - urbanization_data)**2) / \
                            np.sum((urbanization_data - np.mean(urbanization_data))**2)
                
            except Exception:
                return {
                    'status': 'fitting_failed',
                    'message': 'Logistic模型拟合失败',
                    'predicted_year': None,
                    'confidence': 0.0
                }
        else:
            return {
                'status': 'unknown_method',
                'message': f'未知的预测方法: {method}',
                'predicted_year': None,
                'confidence': 0.0
            }
        
        # 判断预测状态
        if predicted_year is None:
            status = 'no_transition_predicted'
        elif predicted_year < years[0]:
            status = 'transition_passed'
        elif predicted_year > years[-1] + self.config.prediction_horizon:
            status = 'transition_far_future'
        else:
            status = 'transition_predicted'
        
        return {
            'status': status,
            'predicted_year': predicted_year,
            'confidence_interval': (year_ci_lower, year_ci_upper),
            'r_squared': r_squared,
            'method': method,
            'current_phase': self.detect_phase(urbanization_data[-1]).phase,
            'years_analyzed': (int(years[0]), int(years[-1]))
        }
    
    def analyze_impact(
        self,
        urbanization_rate: float,
        current_reform_intensity: float
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        分析城镇化对改革的影响
        
        Args:
            urbanization_rate: 当前城镇化率
            current_reform_intensity: 当前改革强度
            
        Returns:
            影响分析结果
        """
        # 相变检测
        phase_result = self.detect_phase(urbanization_rate)
        
        # 耦合效应
        coupling = self.coupling_effect(
            urbanization_rate, 
            current_reform_intensity
        )
        
        # 生成政策建议
        if phase_result.phase == 'pre_transition':
            recommendations = [
                '加快城镇化进程，释放人口红利',
                '在城镇化初期应谨慎推进高强度改革',
                '优先推进城镇化相关基础设施投资'
            ]
        elif phase_result.phase == 'at_transition':
            recommendations = [
                '正处于城镇化相变临界区，改革窗口期',
                '建议把握时机推进渐进式改革',
                '注意改革节奏，避免激化社会矛盾'
            ]
        else:  # post_transition
            recommendations = [
                '城镇化红利释放期，适合深化改革',
                '可以适当加快改革步伐',
                '关注城镇化后社会融合问题'
            ]
        
        return {
            'urbanization_rate': urbanization_rate,
            'phase': phase_result.phase,
            'is_above_critical': phase_result.is_above_critical,
            'coupling_effect': coupling['coupling_effect'],
            'reform_direction': coupling['direction'],
            'interpretation': coupling['interpretation'],
            'recommendations': recommendations
        }
    
    def bootstrap_estimate(
        self,
        urbanization_rates: np.ndarray,
        n_bootstrap: Optional[int] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        Bootstrap重采样估计临界值
        
        Args:
            urbanization_rates: 城镇化率数据
            n_bootstrap: Bootstrap次数
            
        Returns:
            Bootstrap估计结果
        """
        n_boot = n_bootstrap or self.config.n_bootstrap
        n = len(urbanization_rates)
        
        bootstrap_critical_estimates = np.zeros(n_boot)
        
        for i in range(n_boot):
            # 有放回重采样
            indices = self.rng.integers(0, n, size=n)
            sample = urbanization_rates[indices]
            
            # 使用相变点作为临界值估计
            # 简化：使用样本中位数
            bootstrap_critical_estimates[i] = np.median(sample)
        
        return {
            'point_estimate': float(np.mean(bootstrap_critical_estimates)),
            'std_error': float(np.std(bootstrap_critical_estimates)),
            'ci_lower': float(np.percentile(bootstrap_critical_estimates, 2.5)),
            'ci_upper': float(np.percentile(bootstrap_critical_estimates, 97.5)),
            'n_bootstrap': n_boot
        }
    
    def get_phase_boundaries(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """获取相变边界"""
        return {
            'pre_transition_upper': self.config.u_c - self.config.u_c_std,
            'critical_lower': self.config.u_c,
            'critical_upper': self.config.u_c,
            'post_transition_lower': self.config.u_c + self.config.u_c_std
        }
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """生成检测报告"""
        report_lines = [
            "=" * 60,
            "城镇化相变检测报告",
            "=" * 60,
            "",
            "【相变临界值参数】",
            f"  u_c = {self.config.u_c:.1%} ± {self.config.u_c_std:.1%}",
            f"  95% CI: [{(self.config.u_c - 1.96*self.config.u_c_std):.1%}, "
            f"{(self.config.u_c + 1.96*self.config.u_c_std):.1%}]",
            "",
            "【相变边界】",
            f"  前临界区上限: {self.config.u_c - self.config.u_c_std:.1%}",
            f"  临界区: [{self.config.u_c - self.config.u_c_std:.1%}, "
            f"{self.config.u_c + self.config.u_c_std:.1%}]",
            f"  后临界区下限: {self.config.u_c + self.config.u_c_std:.1%}",
            "",
            f"检测历史记录: {len(self._detection_history)} 次",
            "=" * 60
        ]
        return "\n".join(report_lines)
```

#### 12.77.3 使用示例

```python
# 城镇化相变检测
from urbanization_phase_detector import UrbanizationPhaseDetector

# 初始化
detector = UrbanizationPhaseDetector()

# 检测相变
result = detector.detect_phase(urbanization_rate=0.523)
print(f"城镇化率: {result.urbanization_rate:.1%}")
print(f"是否超过临界: {result.is_above_critical}")
print(f"相阶段: {result.phase}")

# 计算耦合效应
coupling = detector.coupling_effect(u=0.523, xi=0.15)
print(f"耦合效应: {coupling['coupling_effect']:.4f}")
print(f"解读: {coupling['interpretation']}")

# 预测相变年份
years = np.array([2010, 2012, 2014, 2016, 2018, 2020, 2022])
urbanization = np.array([0.36, 0.39, 0.42, 0.45, 0.48, 0.51, 0.53])
prediction = detector.phase_transition_year(urbanization, years)
print(f"预测相变年份: {prediction['predicted_year']}")
print(f"R²: {prediction['r_squared']:.4f}")

# 分析影响
analysis = detector.analyze_impact(urbanization_rate=0.523, current_reform_intensity=0.15)
print(f"政策建议: {analysis['recommendations']}")

# Bootstrap估计
bootstrap_result = detector.bootstrap_estimate(urbanization)
print(f"Bootstrap临界值估计: {bootstrap_result['point_estimate']:.1%}")
```

### 12.78 WealthInequalityAnalyzer：财富不平等分析器 ⭐ V3.10.0新增

#### 12.78.1 适配器概述

WealthInequalityAnalyzer实现V3.4.3第3.13.5节的财富不平等分析，支持收入基尼系数与财富基尼系数的分层分析。

**核心参数（来自V3.4.3）**：

| 指标 | 符号 | 数值 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|------|
| 收入基尼系数 | $G_{income}$ | $0.52$ | CFPS 2018数据 |
| 财富基尼系数 | $G_{wealth}$ | $0.86$ | 资本积累"马太效应" |
| 社会不稳定警戒线 | $G_{alert}$ | $0.40$ | 世界银行标准 |

**理论依据**: V3.4.3第2403-2430行

#### 12.78.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.78: WealthInequalityAnalyzer
功能: 财富不平等分析器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.10.0-Alpha.2
理论依据: V3.4.3第3.13.5节财富分布与基尼系数
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from scipy import stats

@dataclass
class InequalityConfig:
    """不平等分析配置"""
    # 基尼系数参数
    g_income: float = 0.52  # 收入基尼
    g_wealth: float = 0.86  # 财富基尼
    g_alert: float = 0.40  # 社会不稳定警戒线
    
    # 财富集中警戒阈值
    wealth_concentration_alert: float = 0.70
    wealth_gap_alert: float = 0.30  # G_wealth - G_income警戒线
    
    # Bootstrap参数
    n_bootstrap: int = 5000
    confidence_level: float = 0.95
    
    # 随机种子
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026

@dataclass
class InequalityResult:
    """不平等分析结果"""
    gini_coefficient: float
    ci_lower: float
    ci_upper: float
    risk_level: str  # 'low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical'
    is_above_alert: bool
    interpretation: str

class WealthInequalityAnalyzer:
    """
    财富不平等分析器
    
    功能特性:
    - 基尼系数计算（收入/财富）
    - 不平等风险评估
    - 贫富差距分析
    - Bootstrap置信区间估计
    
    理论依据: V3.4.3第3.13.5节
    
    核心公式:
    - G_income = Σ|w_i - w_j| / (2N²·w̄)
    - G_wealth = Σ|w_i - w_j| / (2N²·w̄)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[InequalityConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or InequalityConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        self._analysis_history: List[Dict] = []
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        """初始化随机数生成器"""
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
        else:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng()
    
    def calculate_gini(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        use_bootstrap: bool = True
    ) -> InequalityResult:
        """
        计算基尼系数
        
        Args:
            data: 收入或财富数据
            use_bootstrap: 是否使用Bootstrap估计置信区间
            
        Returns:
            基尼系数分析结果
        """
        # 排序数据
        sorted_data = np.sort(data)
        n = len(sorted_data)
        
        # 计算基尼系数（基于公式 G = Σ|w_i - w_j| / (2N²·w̄)）
        cumulative = np.cumsum(sorted_data)
        gini = (2 * np.sum((np.arange(1, n+1) * sorted_data))) / (n * cumulative[-1]) - (n + 1) / n
        
        # Bootstrap置信区间
        if use_bootstrap:
            bootstrap_ginis = self._bootstrap_gini(data)
            ci_lower = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_ginis, 2.5))
            ci_upper = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_ginis, 97.5))
        else:
            ci_lower = gini
            ci_upper = gini
        
        # 风险评估
        risk_level, interpretation = self._assess_risk(gini)
        
        result = InequalityResult(
            gini_coefficient=float(gini),
            ci_lower=ci_lower,
            ci_upper=ci_upper,
            risk_level=risk_level,
            is_above_alert=gini > self.config.g_alert,
            interpretation=interpretation
        )
        
        # 记录分析历史
        self._analysis_history.append({
            'gini': gini,
            'risk_level': risk_level,
            'n_samples': n
        })
        
        return result
    
    def _bootstrap_gini(
        self, 
        data: np.ndarray,
        n_bootstrap: Optional[int] = None
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """Bootstrap重采样估计基尼系数"""
        n_boot = n_bootstrap or self.config.n_bootstrap
        n = len(data)
        
        bootstrap_ginis = np.zeros(n_boot)
        
        for i in range(n_boot):
            indices = self.rng.integers(0, n, size=n)
            bootstrap_sample = data[indices]
            bootstrap_ginis[i] = self._compute_gini_direct(bootstrap_sample)
        
        return bootstrap_ginis
    
    def _compute_gini_direct(self, data: np.ndarray) -> float:
        """直接计算基尼系数"""
        sorted_data = np.sort(data)
        n = len(sorted_data)
        
        if n == 0:
            return 0.0
        
        mean = np.mean(sorted_data)
        if mean == 0:
            return 0.0
        
        # 使用梯形法则
        index = np.arange(1, n + 1)
        gini = (2 * np.sum(index * sorted_data)) / (n * np.sum(sorted_data)) - (n + 1) / n
        
        return float(gini)
    
    def _assess_risk(
        self, 
        gini: float,
        alert_threshold: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """评估不平等风险等级"""
        alert = alert_threshold or self.config.g_alert
        
        if gini < 0.30:
            return 'low', '不平等程度较低，社会流动性良好'
        elif gini < alert:
            return 'medium', '不平等程度中等，需关注收入分配政策'
        elif gini < 0.50:
            return 'high', '不平等程度较高，社会矛盾风险上升'
        elif gini < 0.70:
            return 'very_high', '不平等程度很高，社会流动性严重受阻'
        else:
            return 'critical', '不平等程度极严重，潜在社会动荡风险'
    
    def inequality_risk(
        self,
        gini_coefficient: float,
        gini_type: Literal['income', 'wealth'] = 'income'
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        评估不平等风险等级
        
        Args:
            gini_coefficient: 基尼系数
            gini_type: 基尼类型
            
        Returns:
            风险评估结果
        """
        risk_level, interpretation = self._assess_risk(gini_coefficient)
        
        # 特定类型阈值
        if gini_type == 'wealth':
            concentration_alert = self.config.wealth_concentration_alert
        else:
            concentration_alert = self.config.g_alert
        
        # 风险评分（0-100）
        risk_score = min(100, int(gini_coefficient * 200))
        
        return {
            'gini_coefficient': gini_coefficient,
            'gini_type': gini_type,
            'risk_level': risk_level,
            'risk_score': risk_score,
            'is_above_alert': gini_coefficient > concentration_alert,
            'interpretation': interpretation,
            'alert_threshold': concentration_alert
        }
    
    def wealth_gap_analysis(
        self,
        income_data: np.ndarray,
        wealth_data: np.ndarray
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        贫富差距分析
        
        Args:
            income_data: 收入数据
            wealth_data: 财富数据
            
        Returns:
            贫富差距分析结果
        """
        # 计算两个基尼系数
        income_result = self.calculate_gini(income_data, use_bootstrap=False)
        wealth_result = self.calculate_gini(wealth_data, use_bootstrap=False)
        
        # 计算差距
        gini_gap = wealth_result.gini_coefficient - income_result.gini_coefficient
        
        # Bootstrap估计差距置信区间
        n_bootstrap = self.config.n_bootstrap
        income_ginis = self._bootstrap_gini(income_data, n_bootstrap)
        wealth_ginis = self._bootstrap_gini(wealth_data, n_bootstrap)
        gap_ginis = wealth_ginis - income_ginis
        
        gap_ci_lower = float(np.percentile(gap_ginis, 2.5))
        gap_ci_upper = float(np.percentile(gap_ginis, 97.5))
        
        # 解读
        if gini_gap > self.config.wealth_gap_alert:
            gap_interpretation = '代际财富传递机制过强，机会不平等加剧'
        elif gini_gap > 0.20:
            gap_interpretation = '资本收入增速高于劳动收入，需关注财富分配'
        else:
            gap_interpretation = '收入与财富分布较为匹配'
        
        return {
            'g_income': income_result.gini_coefficient,
            'g_wealth': wealth_result.gini_coefficient,
            'gini_gap': gini_gap,
            'gap_ci_lower': gap_ci_lower,
            'gap_ci_upper': gap_ci_upper,
            'gap_interpretation': gap_interpretation,
            'is_gap_alert': gini_gap > self.config.wealth_gap_alert,
            'income_risk': income_result.risk_level,
            'wealth_risk': wealth_result.risk_level
        }
    
    def lorenz_curve_points(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        n_points: int = 100
    ) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
        """
        计算洛伦兹曲线点
        
        Args:
            data: 收入或财富数据
            n_points: 曲线点数
            
        Returns:
            (population_share, income_share) 元组
        """
        sorted_data = np.sort(data)
        n = len(sorted_data)
        
        # 累积人口比例
        population_share = np.linspace(0, 1, n_points)
        
        # 累积收入比例
        cumulative = np.cumsum(sorted_data)
        income_share = cumulative / cumulative[-1]
        
        # 插值到指定点数
        x = np.linspace(0, 1, n)
        x_new = np.linspace(0, 1, n_points)
        income_share_interp = np.interp(x_new, x, income_share)
        
        return population_share, income_share_interp
    
    def gini_from_lorenz(
        self,
        population_share: np.ndarray,
        income_share: np.ndarray
    ) -> float:
        """
        从洛伦兹曲线计算基尼系数
        
        Args:
            population_share: 人口累积比例
            income_share: 收入累积比例
            
        Returns:
            基尼系数
        """
        # 基尼系数 = 洛伦兹曲线与绝对平等线之间的面积 × 2
        # 使用梯形法则积分
        area_under_lorenz = np.trapz(income_share, population_share)
        area_under_equality = 0.5  # 等腰直角三角形面积
        
        gini = (area_under_equality - area_under_lorenz) / area_under_equality
        
        return float(gini)
    
    def comparative_analysis(
        self,
        data_by_group: Dict[str, np.ndarray]
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        分组比较分析
        
        Args:
            data_by_group: 分组数据字典，键为组名，值为数据数组
            
        Returns:
            分组比较结果
        """
        results = {}
        
        for group_name, data in data_by_group.items():
            if len(data) > 0:
                gini_result = self.calculate_gini(data)
                results[group_name] = {
                    'gini': gini_result.gini_coefficient,
                    'ci': (gini_result.ci_lower, gini_result.ci_upper),
                    'risk_level': gini_result.risk_level,
                    'n_samples': len(data),
                    'mean': float(np.mean(data)),
                    'median': float(np.median(data))
                }
        
        # 按基尼系数排序
        sorted_groups = sorted(results.items(), key=lambda x: x[1]['gini'], reverse=True)
        
        return {
            'groups': results,
            'highest_gini_group': sorted_groups[0][0] if sorted_groups else None,
            'lowest_gini_group': sorted_groups[-1][0] if sorted_groups else None,
            'gini_range': (sorted_groups[-1][1]['gini'], sorted_groups[0][1]['gini']) if sorted_groups else (0, 0)
        }
    
    def policy_indicators(
        self,
        income_data: np.ndarray,
        wealth_data: np.ndarray
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        计算政策指标
        
        Args:
            income_data: 收入数据
            wealth_data: 财富数据
            
        Returns:
            政策指标字典
        """
        income_result = self.calculate_gini(income_data)
        wealth_result = self.calculate_gini(wealth_data)
        
        indicators = {
            'g_income': income_result.gini_coefficient,
            'g_wealth': wealth_result.gini_coefficient,
            'g_income_above_alert': income_result.is_above_alert,
            'g_wealth_above_concentration_alert': wealth_result.gini_coefficient > self.config.wealth_concentration_alert,
            'mobility_index': 1 - income_result.gini_coefficient,  # 简化流动性指数
            'wealth_concentration_ratio': wealth_result.gini_coefficient / income_result.gini_coefficient,
            'policy_recommendations': self._generate_policy_recommendations(
                income_result, wealth_result
            )
        }
        
        return indicators
    
    def _generate_policy_recommendations(
        self,
        income_result: InequalityResult,
        wealth_result: InequalityResult
    ) -> List[str]:
        """生成政策建议"""
        recommendations = []
        
        if income_result.is_above_alert:
            recommendations.append('需关注收入再分配政策')
        
        if wealth_result.gini_coefficient > self.config.wealth_concentration_alert:
            recommendations.append('财富高度集中，可能引发社会流动性下降')
        
        gap = wealth_result.gini_coefficient - income_result.gini_coefficient
        if gap > self.config.wealth_gap_alert:
            recommendations.append('代际财富传递机制过强，机会不平等加剧')
        
        if not recommendations:
            recommendations.append('当前不平等程度在可接受范围内')
        
        return recommendations
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """生成分析报告"""
        report_lines = [
            "=" * 60,
            "财富不平等分析报告",
            "=" * 60,
            "",
            "【基尼系数参数】",
            f"  G_income = {self.config.g_income:.2f} (收入基尼)",
            f"  G_wealth = {self.config.g_wealth:.2f} (财富基尼)",
            f"  G_alert = {self.config.g_alert:.2f} (警戒线)",
            "",
            "【分析统计】",
            f"  分析记录: {len(self._analysis_history)} 次",
            "=" * 60
        ]
        return "\n".join(report_lines)
```

#### 12.78.3 使用示例

```python
# 财富不平等分析
from wealth_inequality_analyzer import WealthInequalityAnalyzer

# 初始化
analyzer = WealthInequalityAnalyzer()

# 计算收入基尼系数
np.random.seed(2026)
income_data = np.random.lognormal(mean=10, sigma=1, size=10000)
result = analyzer.calculate_gini(income_data)
print(f"收入基尼系数: {result.gini_coefficient:.4f}")
print(f"风险等级: {result.risk_level}")

# 评估不平等风险
risk = analyzer.inequality_risk(0.52, 'income')
print(f"风险评分: {risk['risk_score']}")

# 贫富差距分析
wealth_data = np.random.lognormal(mean=12, sigma=1.5, size=10000)
gap_analysis = analyzer.wealth_gap_analysis(income_data, wealth_data)
print(f"基尼差距: {gap_analysis['gini_gap']:.4f}")

# 分组比较

    'urban': np.random.lognormal(mean=11, sigma=1, size=5000),
    'rural': np.random.lognormal(mean=9.5, sigma=1.2, size=5000)
}
comparison = analyzer.comparative_analysis(groups)
print(f"最高基尼组: {comparison['highest_gini_group']}")

# 政策指标
policy = analyzer.policy_indicators(income_data, wealth_data)
print(f"流动性指数: {policy['mobility_index']:.4f}")
```

### 12.79 TailRiskAnalyzer：尾部风险分析器 ⭐ V3.10.0新增

#### 12.79.1 适配器概述

TailRiskAnalyzer实现V3.4.3第3.13.6节的Pareto尾部指数估计与极端风险分析。

**核心参数（来自V3.4.3）**：

| 参数 | 符号 | 数值 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|------|
| Pareto尾部指数 | $\alpha$ | $1.63 \pm 0.20$ | Hill估计量验证 |
| 95%置信区间 | - | $[1.43, 1.83]$ | Bootstrap B=5,000 |

**理论依据**: V3.4.3第2431-2458行

#### 12.79.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.79: TailRiskAnalyzer
功能: 尾部风险分析器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.10.0-Alpha.2
理论依据: V3.4.3第3.13.6节Pareto尾部指数实证
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass
from scipy import stats
from scipy.optimize import brentq

@dataclass
class TailRiskConfig:
    """尾部风险分析配置"""
    # Pareto尾部指数
    alpha: float = 1.63
    alpha_std: float = 0.20
    
    # Hill估计参数
    k_hill: int = 500  # 使用尾部数量
    n_bootstrap: int = 5000  # Bootstrap次数
    
    # 风险价值参数
    var_confidence_levels: List[float] = [0.95, 0.99]
    
    # 极端阈值（财富分布的上分位数）
    extreme_quantile: float = 0.95
    
    # 随机种子
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026

@dataclass
class TailIndexResult:
    """尾部指数估计结果"""
    alpha: float
    std_error: float
    ci_lower: float
    ci_upper: float
    k_hill: int  # 使用的尾部观测数
    interpretation: str

@dataclass
class ExtremeRiskResult:
    """极端风险分析结果"""
    threshold: float
    exceedance_probability: float
    expected_exceedance: float
    var_estimates: Dict[float, float]
    interpretation: str

class TailRiskAnalyzer:
    """
    尾部风险分析器
    
    功能特性:
    - Pareto尾部指数Hill估计
    - Bootstrap置信区间
    - 极端风险概率计算
    - 风险价值(VaR)分析
    
    理论依据: V3.4.3第3.13.6节
    
    核心公式:
    - P(w > x) ~ x^(-α), x → ∞
    - α = 1.63 ± 0.20 (Hill估计)
    - G = 1/(2α - 1), α > 0.5
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[TailRiskConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or TailRiskConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        self._analysis_history: List[Dict] = []
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        """初始化随机数生成器"""
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
        else:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng()
    
    def estimate_tail_index(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        method: str = 'hill',
        adaptive_k: bool = False
    ) -> TailIndexResult:
        """
        估计Pareto尾部指数
        
        Args:
            data: 财富/收入数据
            method: 估计方法 ('hill', 'mle', 'pickands')
            adaptive_k: 是否自适应选择k
            
        Returns:
            尾部指数估计结果
        """
        if method == 'hill':
            alpha, std_error, k = self._hill_estimator(data, adaptive_k)
        elif method == 'mle':
            alpha, std_error, k = self._mle_estimator(data)
        elif method == 'pickands':
            alpha, std_error, k = self._pickands_estimator(data)
        else:
            raise ValueError(f"未知的估计方法: {method}")
        
        # Bootstrap置信区间
        bootstrap_alphas = self._bootstrap_tail_index(data, method)
        ci_lower = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_alphas, 2.5))
        ci_upper = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_alphas, 97.5))
        
        # 解读
        interpretation = self._interpret_alpha(alpha)
        
        result = TailIndexResult(
            alpha=alpha,
            std_error=std_error,
            ci_lower=ci_lower,
            ci_upper=ci_upper,
            k_hill=k,
            interpretation=interpretation
        )
        
        # 记录分析历史
        self._analysis_history.append({
            'alpha': alpha,
            'method': method,
            'k_hill': k
        })
        
        return result
    
    def _hill_estimator(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        adaptive: bool = False
    ) -> Tuple[float, float, int]:
        """
        Hill估计量
        
        α = 1 / (1/k * Σ log(X_i / X_(k+1)))
        """
        sorted_data = np.sort(data)[::-1]  # 降序排列
        n = len(sorted_data)
        
        # 选择k值
        if adaptive:
            k = self._select_optimal_k(sorted_data)
        else:
            k = min(self.config.k_hill, n - 1)
        
        if k < 10:
            return self.config.alpha, 0.0, k
        
        # Hill估计
        log_ratios = np.log(sorted_data[:k] / sorted_data[k])
        alpha_inv = np.mean(log_ratios)
        alpha = 1.0 / alpha_inv if alpha_inv > 0 else self.config.alpha
        
        # 标准误
        variance = np.var(log_ratios) / (k * alpha_inv**4)
        std_error = float(np.sqrt(variance)) if variance > 0 else 0.0
        
        return alpha, std_error, k
    
    def _select_optimal_k(self, sorted_data: np.ndarray) -> int:
        """自适应选择最优k值"""
        n = len(sorted_data)
        max_k = min(self.config.k_hill, n - 1)
        
        # 使用二阶导数选择拐点
        alpha_values = []
        k_values = list(range(50, max_k, 10))
        
        for k in k_values:
            log_ratios = np.log(sorted_data[:k] / sorted_data[k])
            if np.mean(log_ratios) > 0:
                alpha = 1.0 / np.mean(log_ratios)
                alpha_values.append(alpha)
            else:
                alpha_values.append(np.inf)
        
        # 选择alpha最稳定的k
        if len(alpha_values) > 2:
            second_diff = np.diff(np.diff(alpha_values))
            # 选择二阶导最小的点（稳定性最高）
            optimal_idx = np.argmin(np.abs(second_diff)) + 1
            return k_values[min(optimal_idx, len(k_values)-1)]
        
        return max_k // 2
    
    def _mle_estimator(self, data: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float, int]:
        """极大似然估计（Pareto分布）"""
        sorted_data = np.sort(data)
        threshold = np.percentile(sorted_data, self.config.extreme_quantile * 100)
        tail_data = sorted_data[sorted_data > threshold]
        
        n_tail = len(tail_data)
        if n_tail < 10:
            return self.config.alpha, 0.0, n_tail
        
        # MLE: α = n / Σ log(x_i / threshold)
        alpha = n_tail / np.sum(np.log(tail_data / threshold))
        
        # 标准误
        std_error = alpha / np.sqrt(n_tail)
        
        return alpha, std_error, n_tail
    
    def _pickands_estimator(self, data: np.ndarray) -> Tuple[float, float, int]:
        """Pickands估计量"""
        sorted_data = np.sort(data)[::-1]
        n = len(sorted_data)
        k = min(self.config.k_hill // 4, n // 4 - 1)
        
        if k < 5:
            return self.config.alpha, 0.0, 0
        
        # Pickands估计
        try:
            log_ratio1 = np.log(sorted_data[3*k] / sorted_data[4*k])
            log_ratio2 = np.log(sorted_data[k] / sorted_data[2*k])
            
            if log_ratio1 > 0 and log_ratio2 > 0:
                alpha = np.log(log_ratio1 / log_ratio2) / np.log(2)
                alpha = max(0.5, min(5.0, alpha))  # 限制范围
            else:
                alpha = self.config.alpha
            
            std_error = alpha * 0.5  # 简化标准误
            
        except:
            alpha = self.config.alpha
            std_error = 0.0
        
        return alpha, std_error, 4*k
    
    def _bootstrap_tail_index(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        method: str,
        n_bootstrap: Optional[int] = None
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """Bootstrap重采样估计尾部指数"""
        n_boot = n_bootstrap or self.config.n_bootstrap
        n = len(data)
        
        bootstrap_alphas = np.zeros(n_boot)
        
        for i in range(n_boot):
            indices = self.rng.integers(0, n, size=n)
            bootstrap_sample = data[indices]
            
            try:
                if method == 'hill':
                    alpha, _, _ = self._hill_estimator(bootstrap_sample)
                else:
                    alpha, _, _ = self._mle_estimator(bootstrap_sample)
                
                bootstrap_alphas[i] = alpha
            except:
                bootstrap_alphas[i] = self.config.alpha
        
        return bootstrap_alphas
    
    def _interpret_alpha(self, alpha: float) -> str:
        """解读尾部指数含义"""
        if alpha < 1.0:
            return '方差无穷，极端厚尾风险极高'
        elif alpha < 1.5:
            return '方差无穷，厚尾特征显著'
        elif alpha < 2.0:
            return '方差无穷，厚尾特征明显（典型新兴经济体）'
        elif alpha < 2.5:
            return '方差有限，尾部相对较薄'
        else:
            return '尾部衰减较快，风险相对可控'
    
    def extreme_risk_prob(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        threshold: Optional[float] = None,
        alpha: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        计算极端风险概率
        
        P(w > x) ~ x^(-α)
        
        Args:
            data: 财富数据
            threshold: 极端阈值（可选）
            alpha: 尾部指数（可选）
            
        Returns:
            极端风险概率
        """
        if alpha is None:
            tail_result = self.estimate_tail_index(data)
            alpha = tail_result.alpha
        
        if threshold is None:
            threshold = np.percentile(data, self.config.extreme_quantile * 100)
        
        # 使用Pareto分布计算超过阈值概率
        # P(w > x) = (threshold / x)^alpha
        
        return {
            'threshold': threshold,
            'alpha': alpha,
            'quantile_99': threshold * (1 - 0.99)**(-1/alpha) if alpha > 0 else 0,
            'quantile_999': threshold * (1 - 0.999)**(-1/alpha) if alpha > 0 else 0,
            'interpretation': f'超过{threshold:.2f}的概率可通过Pareto模型估计'
        }
    
    def var_analysis(
        self,
        data: np.ndarray,
        confidence_levels: Optional[List[float]] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        风险价值(VaR)分析
        
        Args:
            data: 财富/收益数据
            confidence_levels: 置信水平列表
            
        Returns:
            VaR分析结果
        """
        if confidence_levels is None:
            confidence_levels = self.config.var_confidence_levels
        
        # 经验VaR
        empirical_vars = {}
        for cl in confidence_levels:
            var = float(np.percentile(data, (1 - cl) * 100))
            empirical_vars[f'{int(cl*100)}%'] = var
        
        # Pareto尾部VaR（对极端尾部使用Pareto拟合）
        sorted_data = np.sort(data)
        threshold = np.percentile(sorted_data, self.config.extreme_quantile * 100)
        tail_data = sorted_data[sorted_data >= threshold]
        
        alpha, _, _ = self._mle_estimator(data)
        
        pareto_vars = {}
        for cl in confidence_levels:
            if cl > self.config.extreme_quantile:
                # 使用Pareto分布
                excess_prob = cl - self.config.extreme_quantile
                var = threshold * (excess_prob / (1 - self.config.extreme_quantile))**(-1/alpha)
                pareto_vars[f'{int(cl*100)}%'] = float(var)
            else:
                pareto_vars[f'{int(cl*100)}%'] = empirical_vars[f'{int(cl*100)}%']
        
        # 条件VaR（Expected Shortfall）
        cvar_95 = float(np.mean(sorted_data[sorted_data <= empirical_vars['95%']]))
        cvar_99 = float(np.mean(sorted_data[sorted_data <= empirical_vars['99%']]))
        
        return {
            'empirical_var': empirical_vars,
            'pareto_var': pareto_vars,
            'cvar_95': cvar_95,
            'cvar_99': cvar_99,
            'alpha': alpha,
            'threshold': threshold
        }
    
    def gini_tail_relationship(
        self,
        alpha: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """
        分析尾部指数与基尼系数关系
        
        理论关系: G = 1/(2α - 1), α > 0.5
        
        Args:
            alpha: 尾部指数
            
        Returns:
            理论基尼系数
        """
        if alpha is None:
            alpha = self.config.alpha
        
        if alpha > 0.5:
            gini_theoretical = 1.0 / (2 * alpha - 1)
        else:
            gini_theoretical = np.inf
        
        # 实际值对比
        gini_income = self.config.g_income  # 0.52
        gini_wealth = self.config.g_wealth  # 0.86
        
        # 差距分析
        gap_income = gini_income - gini_theoretical if gini_theoretical != np.inf else np.inf
        gap_wealth = gini_wealth - gini_theoretical if gini_theoretical != np.inf else np.inf
        
        return {
            'alpha': alpha,
            'gini_theoretical': float(gini_theoretical) if gini_theoretical != np.inf else 'inf',
            'gini_income': gini_income,
            'gini_wealth': gini_wealth,
            'gap_income': float(gap_income) if isinstance(gap_income, float) else gap_income,
            'gap_wealth': float(gap_wealth) if isinstance(gap_wealth, float) else gap_wealth,
            'interpretation': self._interpret_gini_gap(gap_income, gap_wealth)
        }
    
    def _interpret_gini_gap(
        self,
        gap_income: float,
        gap_wealth: float
    ) -> str:
        """解读基尼差距"""
        if isinstance(gap_income, float) and gap_income > 0.3:
            income_interp = '收入分布由非Pareto机制主导'
        elif isinstance(gap_income, float):
            income_interp = '收入分布基本符合Pareto假设'
        else:
            income_interp = '无法计算'
        
        if isinstance(gap_wealth, float) and gap_wealth > 0.3:
            wealth_interp = '财富集中由遗产继承、房产增值等非Pareto机制主导'
        elif isinstance(gap_wealth, float):
            wealth_interp = '财富分布基本符合Pareto假设'
        else:
            wealth_interp = '无法计算'
        
        return f'{income_interp}；{wealth_interp}'
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """生成分析报告"""
        report_lines = [
            "=" * 60,
            "尾部风险分析报告",
            "=" * 60,
            "",
            "【Pareto尾部指数参数】",
            f"  α = {self.config.alpha:.2f} ± {self.config.alpha_std:.2f}",
            f"  95% CI: [{self.config.alpha - 1.96*self.config.alpha_std:.2f}, "
            f"{self.config.alpha + 1.96*self.config.alpha_std:.2f}]",
            "",
            "【经济含义】",
            f"  α ∈ [1.5, 2.0]: 典型新兴经济体区间",
            f"  当前α = {self.config.alpha:.2f}: {self._interpret_alpha(self.config.alpha)}",
            "",
            f"分析历史记录: {len(self._analysis_history)} 次",
            "=" * 60
        ]
        return "\n".join(report_lines)
```

#### 12.79.3 使用示例

```python
# 尾部风险分析
from tail_risk_analyzer import TailRiskAnalyzer

# 初始化
analyzer = TailRiskAnalyzer()

# 生成模拟财富数据（对数正态分布模拟财富）
np.random.seed(2026)
wealth_data = np.random.lognormal(mean=11, sigma=1.5, size=100000)

# 估计尾部指数
result = analyzer.estimate_tail_index(wealth_data, method='hill', adaptive_k=True)
print(f"尾部指数: α = {result.alpha:.4f} ± {result.std_error:.4f}")
print(f"95% CI: [{result.ci_lower:.4f}, {result.ci_upper:.4f}]")
print(f"解读: {result.interpretation}")

# 极端风险概率
risk = analyzer.extreme_risk_prob(wealth_data, threshold=1000000)
print(f"99%分位数: {risk['quantile_99']:.2f}")
print(f"99.9%分位数: {risk['quantile_999']:.2f}")

# VaR分析
var_result = analyzer.var_analysis(wealth_data)
print(f"95% VaR: {var_result['empirical_var']['95%']:.2f}")
print(f"99% VaR: {var_result['pareto_var']['99%']:.2f}")
print(f"95% CVaR: {var_result['cvar_95']:.2f}")

# 基尼-尾部关系
gini_rel = analyzer.gini_tail_relationship(alpha=result.alpha)
print(f"理论基尼: {gini_rel['gini_theoretical']:.4f}")
print(f"收入基尼: {gini_rel['gini_income']:.4f}")
print(f"财富基尼: {gini_rel['gini_wealth']:.4f}")
```

### 12.80 OpennessRatioAdapter：开闭比适配器 ⭐ V3.10.0新增

#### 12.80.1 适配器概述

OpennessRatioAdapter实现V3.4.3第3.12.3节的开闭比计算与系统稳态分类。

**核心参数（来自V3.4.3）**：

| 参数 | 符号 | 数值 | 说明 |
|------|------|------|------|
| 开闭比 | $\mathcal{R}$ | $\frac{\sum \Gamma_m}{\sum \gamma_k}$ | 外部/内部跳跃强度比 |
| 封闭主导型阈值 | $\mathcal{R} < 0.1$ | - | 内部退相干主导 |
| 开放耦合型阈值 | $0.1 \leq \mathcal{R} \leq 1$ | - | 内外耦合平衡 |
| 外部驱动型阈值 | $\mathcal{R} > 1$ | - | 外部冲击主导 |
| 家庭层面开闭比 | $\mathcal{R}_{family}$ | $4.0 \pm 0.5$ | 微观实证 |

**理论依据**: V3.4.3第2276-2301行

#### 12.80.2 核心代码实现

```python
"""
适配器12.80: OpennessRatioAdapter
功能: 开闭比适配器
作者: 彭耀成
版本: V3.10.0-Alpha.2
理论依据: V3.4.3第3.12.3节开放系统稳态分类器
"""

import numpy as np
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Literal
from dataclasses import dataclass
from scipy import stats

@dataclass
class OpennessConfig:
    """开闭比配置"""
    # 系统类型阈值
    closed_threshold: float = 0.1
    open_threshold: float = 1.0
    
    # 微观层参数（家庭）
    r_family_mean: float = 4.0
    r_family_std: float = 0.5
    
    # Bootstrap参数
    n_bootstrap: int = 5000
    confidence_level: float = 0.95
    
    # 随机种子
    random_seed: Optional[int] = 2026

@dataclass
class SystemTypeResult:
    """系统类型分类结果"""
    openness_ratio: float
    system_type: str  # 'closed_dominant', 'open_coupled', 'externally_driven'
    stability: str  # 'stable', 'transitional', 'unstable'
    confidence: float
    ci_lower: float
    ci_upper: float

class OpennessRatioAdapter:
    """
    开闭比适配器
    
    功能特性:
    - 开闭比计算
    - 系统稳态分类
    - 稳定性评估
    - Bootstrap置信区间
    
    理论依据: V3.4.3第3.12.3节
    
    核心公式:
    - ℛ = ΣΓ_m / Σγ_k（外部/内部跳跃强度比）
    - ℛ < 0.1: 封闭主导型
    - 0.1 ≤ ℛ ≤ 1: 开放耦合型（最优）
    - ℛ > 1: 外部驱动型（失控）
    """
    
    def __init__(self, config: Optional[OpennessConfig] = None):
        self.config = config or OpennessConfig()
        self._initialize_rng()
        self._classification_history: List[Dict] = []
        
    def _initialize_rng(self) -> None:
        """初始化随机数生成器"""
        if self.config.random_seed is not None:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng(self.config.random_seed)
        else:
            self.rng = np.random.default_rng()
    
    def calculate_openness_ratio(
        self,
        internal_strength: float,
        external_strength: float,
        use_bootstrap: bool = True
    ) -> SystemTypeResult:
        """
        计算开闭比
        
        Args:
            internal_strength: 内部跳跃总强度 Σγ_k
            external_strength: 外部跳跃总强度 ΣΓ_m
            use_bootstrap: 是否使用Bootstrap
            
        Returns:
            系统类型分类结果
        """
        # 计算开闭比
        if internal_strength > 0:
            r = external_strength / internal_strength
        else:
            r = np.inf if external_strength > 0 else 0
        
        # 分类系统类型
        system_type, stability = self._classify_system(r)
        
        # Bootstrap置信区间
        if use_bootstrap:
            bootstrap_r = self._bootstrap_openness(internal_strength, external_strength)
            ci_lower = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_r, 2.5))
            ci_upper = float(np.percentile(bootstrap_r, 97.5))
        else:
            ci_lower = r
            ci_upper = r
        
        result = SystemTypeResult(
            openness_ratio=float(r),
            system_type=system_type,
            stability=stability,
            confidence=0.95 if use_bootstrap else 0.70,
            ci_lower=ci_lower,
            ci_upper=ci_upper
        )
        
        # 记录分类历史
        self._classification_history.append({
            'r': r,
            'system_type': system_type,
            'stability': stability
        })
        
        return result
    
    def _bootstrap_openness(
        self,
        internal: float,
        external: float,
        n_bootstrap: Optional[int] = None
    ) -> np.ndarray:
        """Bootstrap估计开闭比"""
        n_boot = n_bootstrap or self.config.n_bootstrap
        
        # 假设强度服从正态分布（简化）
        internal_std = internal * 0.1 if internal > 0 else 0.1
        external_std = external * 0.1 if external > 0 else 0.1
        
        bootstrap_r = np.zeros(n_boot)
        
        for i in range(n_boot):
            int_sample = max(0.01, self.rng.normal(internal, internal_std))
            ext_sample = max(0, self.rng.normal(external, external_std))
            bootstrap_r[i] = ext_sample / int_sample
        
        # 处理无穷值
        bootstrap_r = np.clip(bootstrap_r, 0, 100)
        
        return bootstrap_r
    
    def _classify_system(
        self,
        r: float
    ) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """
        分类系统类型
        
        Returns:
            (system_type, stability)
        """
        if r < self.config.closed_threshold:
            system_type = 'closed_dominant'
            stability = 'stable'
        elif r > self.config.open_threshold:
            system_type = 'externally_driven'
            stability = 'unstable'
        else:
            system_type = 'open_coupled'
            stability = 'stable'
        
        return system_type, stability
    
    def classify_system_type(
        self,
        r: float
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        分类系统类型
        
        Args:
            r: 开闭比
            
        Returns:
            分类结果字典
        """
        system_type, stability = self._classify_system(r)
        
        type_descriptions = {
            'closed_dominant': {
                'name': '封闭主导型',
                'characteristics': [
                    '内部退相干主导',
                    '稳态由内部决定',
                    '系统自主性强'
                ],
                'examples': ['明末', '传统组织', '自给自足经济体']
            },
            'open_coupled': {
                'name': '开放耦合型（最优）',
                'characteristics': [
                    '内外耦合平衡',
                    '存在竞争稳态',
                    '适应性最强'
                ],
                'examples': ['洋务时期', '改革开放', '现代企业']
            },
            'externally_driven': {
                'name': '外部驱动型（失控）',
                'characteristics': [
                    '外部冲击主导',
                    '系统无自治稳态',
                    '高度不确定性'
                ],
                'examples': ['清末(1895后)', '战乱国家', '新兴市场危机']
            }
        }
        
        stability_descriptions = {
            'stable': '系统处于稳态，可预测性强',
            'transitional': '系统处于过渡态，谨防滑向失控',
            'unstable': '系统不稳定，需要干预'
        }
        
        return {
            'r': r,
            'system_type': system_type,
            'type_info': type_descriptions[system_type],
            'stability': stability,
            'stability_description': stability_descriptions[stability]
        }
    
    def stability_assessment(
        self,
        r: float,
        time_horizon: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        稳定性评估
        
        Args:
            r: 开闭比
            time_horizon: 评估时间范围
            
        Returns:
            稳定性评估结果
        """
        assessment = self.classify_system_type(r)
        
        # 稳定性评分
        if r < self.config.closed_threshold:
            stability_score = 100 - r * 500  # 封闭程度越高越稳定
        elif r > self.config.open_threshold:
            stability_score = max(0, 100 - (r - 1) * 50)  # 外部驱动越强越不稳定
        else:
            stability_score = 90 - abs(r - 0.5) * 20  # 接近0.5最稳定
        
        # 风险等级
        if r < self.config.closed_threshold:
            risk_level = 'low'
            risk_description = '风险较低，自治能力强'
        elif r < 0.3:
            risk_level = 'medium'
            risk_description = '风险中等，需关注外部依赖'
        elif r < 1.0:
            risk_level = 'medium_high'
            risk_description = '风险较高，外部依赖明显'
        else:
            risk_level = 'high'
            risk_description = '风险很高，需要干预降低外部依赖'
        
        # 政策建议
        if risk_level == 'low':
            recommendations = ['保持现有开放水平', '关注内部效率提升']
        elif risk_level == 'medium':
            recommendations = ['适度增加内部能力建设', '控制外部依赖增长']
        elif risk_level == 'medium_high':
            recommendations = ['加快核心技术自主可控', '降低关键环节外部依赖']
        else:
            recommendations = ['紧急制定降外部依赖战略', '建立内部替代能力', '控制外部风险敞口']
        
        return {
            'r': r,
            'stability_score': stability_score,
            'risk_level': risk_level,
            'risk_description': risk_description,
            'recommendations': recommendations
        }
    
    def analyze_family_openness(
        self,
        internal_strength: float,
        external_strength: float
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        分析家庭层面开闭比
        
        Args:
            internal_strength: 家庭内部收入/资源
            external_strength: 家庭外部依赖（市场收入、转移支付等）
            
        Returns:
            家庭层面分析结果
        """
        r = external_strength / internal_strength if internal_strength > 0 else np.inf
        
        # 家庭类型分类
        if r < 0.1:
            family_type = '封闭型'
            description = '自给自足农业家庭，外部依赖极低'
        elif r <= 1:
            family_type = '开放耦合型'
            description = '工薪阶层家庭，工资收入为主'
        else:
            family_type = '外部驱动型'
            description = '高脆弱性家庭，市场风险敞口大'
        
        # 与实证均值对比
        r_mean = self.config.r_family_mean
        z_score = (r - r_mean) / self.config.r_family_std if self.config.r_family_std > 0 else 0
        
        return {
            'r': r,
            'family_type': family_type,
            'description': description,
            'r_mean': r_mean,
            'z_score': z_score,
            'interpretation': f'Z-score = {z_score:.2f}，{"高于" if z_score > 0 else "低于"}家庭层面均值'
        }
    
    def temporal_analysis(
        self,
        internal_series: np.ndarray,
        external_series: np.ndarray,
        time_points: np.ndarray
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        时间序列分析
        
        Args:
            internal_series: 内部强度时间序列
            external_series: 外部强度时间序列
            time_points: 时间点
            
        Returns:
            时间序列分析结果
        """
        r_series = external_series / np.maximum(internal_series, 0.01)
        
        # 统计
        r_mean = float(np.mean(r_series))
        r_std = float(np.std(r_series))
        r_trend = self._compute_trend(time_points, r_series)
        
        # 分类变化
        types = [self._classify_system(r)[0] for r in r_series]
        type_changes = sum(1 for i in range(1, len(types)) if types[i] != types[i-1])
        
        # 稳定性评估
        if r_trend > 0.1:
            trend_interpretation = '开闭比上升，外部依赖增强'
        elif r_trend < -0.1:
            trend_interpretation = '开闭比下降，内部能力增强'
        else:
            trend_interpretation = '开闭比基本稳定'
        
        return {
            'r_series': r_series.tolist(),
            'r_mean': r_mean,
            'r_std': r_std,
            'r_trend': r_trend,
            'trend_interpretation': trend_interpretation,
            'type_changes': type_changes,
            'initial_type': types[0],
            'final_type': types[-1]
        }
    
    def _compute_trend(
        self,
        x: np.ndarray,
        y: np.ndarray
    ) -> float:
        """计算趋势斜率"""
        if len(x) < 2 or len(y) < 2:
            return 0.0
        
        slope, _, _, _, _ = stats.linregress(x, y)
        return float(slope)
    
    def coupling_strength_analysis(
        self,
        internal_strength: float,
        external_strength: float,
        coherence: Optional[float] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        耦合强度分析
        
        Args:
            internal_strength: 内部强度
            external_strength: 外部强度
            coherence: 相干系数（可选）
            
        Returns:
            耦合分析结果
        """
        r = external_strength / internal_strength if internal_strength > 0 else 0
        
        # 计算耦合强度
        coupling_strength = min(1.0, r / self.config.open_threshold)
        
        # 相干效应
        if coherence is not None:
            if coherence > 0:
                effect = '协同增强'
                description = '外部冲击效果被放大'
            elif coherence < 0:
                effect = '相互抑制'
                description = '外部冲击效果被削弱'
            else:
                effect = '无相干'
                description = '内外冲击独立作用'
        else:
            effect = 'unknown'
            description = '相干系数未知'
        
        return {
            'r': r,
            'coupling_strength': coupling_strength,
            'coherence': coherence,
            'coherence_effect': effect,
            'interpretation': description
        }
    
    def get_thresholds(self) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """获取分类阈值"""
        return {
            'closed_threshold': self.config.closed_threshold,
            'optimal_range_lower': self.config.closed_threshold,
            'optimal_range_upper': self.config.open_threshold,
            'externally_driven_threshold': self.config.open_threshold
        }
    
    def generate_report(self) -> str:
        """生成分析报告"""
        report_lines = [
            "=" * 60,
            "开闭比分析报告",
            "=" * 60,
            "",
            "【分类阈值】",
            f"  封闭主导型: ℛ < {self.config.closed_threshold:.1f}",
            f"  开放耦合型: {self.config.closed_threshold:.1f} ≤ ℛ ≤ {self.config.open_threshold:.1f}",
            f"  外部驱动型: ℛ > {self.config.open_threshold:.1f}",
            "",
            "【家庭层面参数】",
            f"  ℛ_family = {self.config.r_family_mean:.1f} ± {self.config.r_family_std:.1f}",
            "",
            f"分类历史记录: {len(self._classification_history)} 次",
            "=" * 60
        ]
        return "\n".join(report_lines)
```

#### 12.80.3 使用示例

```python
# 开闭比分析
from openness_ratio_adapter import OpennessRatioAdapter

# 初始化
adapter = OpennessRatioAdapter()

# 计算开闭比
internal_strength = 0.8  # 内部退相干强度
external_strength = 0.3  # 外部冲击强度
result = adapter.calculate_openness_ratio(internal_strength, external_strength)
print(f"开闭比: {result.openness_ratio:.4f}")
print(f"系统类型: {result.system_type}")
print(f"稳定性: {result.stability}")

# 分类系统
classification = adapter.classify_system_type(r=0.4)
print(f"类型名称: {classification['type_info']['name']}")
print(f"特征: {classification['type_info']['characteristics']}")

# 稳定性评估
assessment = adapter.stability_assessment(r=0.4)
print(f"稳定性评分: {assessment['stability_score']:.1f}")
print(f"风险等级: {assessment['risk_level']}")
print(f"建议: {assessment['recommendations']}")

# 家庭层面分析
family_result = adapter.analyze_family_openness(
    internal_strength=20000,  # 农业产出
    external_strength=80000   # 打工收入
)
print(f"家庭类型: {family_result['family_type']}")

# 时间序列分析
years = np.array([2015, 2016, 2017, 2018, 2019, 2020])
internal = np.array([0.8, 0.75, 0.7, 0.65, 0.6, 0.55])
external = np.array([0.2, 0.25, 0.3, 0.4, 0.5, 0.65])
temporal = adapter.temporal_analysis(internal, external, years)
print(f"开闭比趋势: {temporal['r_trend']:.4f}")
print(f"类型变化次数: {temporal['type_changes']}")

# 耦合分析
coupling = adapter.coupling_strength_analysis(0.8, 0.3, coherence=0.3)
print(f"耦合强度: {coupling['coupling_strength']:.4f}")
```

### H.2 适配器专用参数

| 适配器ID | 参数名称 | 符号 | 默认值 | 校准方法 |
|---------|---------|------|-------|---------|
| 12.1 | EDR阈值 | EDR_thresh | 0.15 | 分位数分析 |
| 12.1 | CDL阈值 | CDL_thresh | 0.20 | 业务定义 |
| 12.2 | 消息类型数 | n_types | 11 | 分类体系 |
| 12.3 | 枢纽节点阈值 | hub_thresh | 0.3 | 网络特性 |
| 12.4 | 告警阈值 | alert_thresh | 2.0 | 业务SLA |
| 12.5 | OU漂移参数 | θ | 0.5 | 极大似然 |

| 12.9 | 干预窗口 | window | 3年 | 政策周期 |

| 12.75 | 集成方法 | method | 'weighted' | 交叉验证 |

### H.3 参数敏感性边界

| 参数 | 下界 | 上界 | 边界类型 | 边界依据 |
|-----|-----|-----|---------|---------|
| G_baseline | 0.01 | 0.15 | 硬边界 | 经济学共识 |
| α | 0.25 | 0.65 | 硬边界 | Cobb-Douglas约束 |
| ξ_c | 0.30 | 0.60 | 软边界 | 改革实践 |
| γ₁ | 0.5 | 2.0 | 软边界 | 历史拟合 |
| β | 0.1 | 1.0 | 硬边界 | OU过程约束 |
| α_熵 | 0.1 | 0.6 | 软边界 | 优化经验 |

### K.4 使用说明

**快速查询流程**:
1. 根据任务需求确定功能关键词
2. 在K.1核心功能关键词映射中查找
3. 获取推荐适配器编号和名称
4. 在正文中查阅该适配器的详细实现

**示例查询**:
- 需求: "分析历史事件的因果关系" → 关键词"因果分析" → 12.9 HistoricalCausalAnalyzer
- 需求: "评估改革对财富分配的影响" → 关键词"改革分析" → 12.27, 12.80, 12.4.2
- 需求: "检测城镇化是否达到临界点" → 关键词"城镇化" → 12.77 UrbanizationPhaseDetector

- EFPF模型: 基于帕累托前沿理论和信息熵理论的创新应用
- **V4.5.0扩展理论**: CE-C1~C4因果层级、涌现EI(Effective Information)、传递熵TE、渗流理论、ABM、排队论M/M/c、DEA数据包络分析、Kalman滤波、HMM隐马尔可夫模型

**技术参考**:
- Python科学计算栈: NumPy, SciPy, Pandas
- 统计检验方法: Bootstrap, Monte Carlo, Granger因果检验
- 网络分析: NetworkX图论算法库
- **V4.5.0新增**: statsmodels(ARIMA/BSTS), pgmpy(贝叶斯网络), Mesa(ABM), scikit-learn

**验证参考**:
- 5次端到端真实验证(清末崩溃案例)
- 200+个单元测试用例(100%通过)
- MECE/EFPF/八维度三重验证体系
- **V4.5.0新增**: 全量回归测试23 adapter×1 core method全部通过, 可视化工具集8脚本
